### ΠΕΡΙ ΤΟΥ ΡΜ΄ ΨΑΛΜΟΥ
### «ΚΥΡΙΕ ΕΚΕΚΡΑΞΑ»

**6.** Ἐν τοῖς καθ᾿ ἑκάστην ἡμέραν ψαλλομένοις Ἑσπερινοῖς τῶν Ψαλμῶν **«Κύριε ἐκέκραξα»**⁵, **«Φωνῇ μου πρὸς Κύριον»**, **«Ἐκ βαθέων ἐκέκραξά σοι»**, καὶ τὸ **«Αἰνεῖτε τὸν Κύριον»**, ψάλλονται πλήρεις οἱ στίχοι εἰς τοὺς διατεταγμένους ἤχους. Ἐν δὲ τοῖς Ἑσπερινοῖς τῶν Μ. ἑορτῶν εἴθισται πρὸς συντομίαν νὰ παραλείπωνται οἱ στίχοι ἀπὸ τοῦ **«Θοῦ Κύριε»** καὶ ἐφεξῆς, καὶ νὰ μεταβαίνῃ ὁ πρῶτος χορὸς εἰς τὸν στίχον τὸν ἁρμόδιον ἀναλόγως τῆς ποσότητος τῶν ἑπομένων στιχηρῶν.

---

### ΠΕΡΙ ΤΩΝ ΠΡΩΤΩΝ ΕΣΠΕΡΙΩΝ ΣΤΙΧΗΡΩΝ

**7.** Εἰς πάντα Ἑσπερινὸν τοῦ Σαββάτου πρὸς τὴν Κυριακὴν καὶ πάντα Ὄρθρον ἑκάστης Κυριακῆς τοῦ ὅλου ἐνιαυτοῦ, ψάλλονται ἀπαραιτήτως τὰ τῆς Ὀκτωήχου Ἀναστάσιμα, ἤτοι Στιχηρά, Δοξαστικά, Ἀπολυτίκια, Τριαδικά, Καθίσματα, Ὑπακοαί, Ἀντίφωνα, Κανόνες, Κοντάκια, Οἶκοι, Ἐξαποστειλάρια, Ἑωθινά, Μακαρισμοὶ κτλ., ἅτινα καὶ προηγοῦνται τῶν Ἀκολουθιῶν τῶν Θεομητορικῶν ἑορτῶν καὶ τῶν Ἀποδόσεων αὐτῶν ὡς καὶ τῶν Ἀποδόσεων τῶν Δεσποτικῶν ἑορτῶν,

---

⁴ Σημειωτέον ὅτι αἱ δύο αὗται ἑορταί, ὡς μετέχουσαι καὶ τοῦ χαρακτῆρος Δεσποτικῶν ἑορτῶν, ἐμφαίνουσί τι ἐξαιρετικόν· διὸ καὶ κατὰ πολλὰ μέρη ἐν ταῖς Ἀκολουθίαις αὐτῶν παρατηρεῖται ἡ ἀπὸ τῶν ἄλλων Θεομητορικῶν ἑορτῶν ὑπεροχὴ αὐτῶν.  
⁵ Ὅτε ἄρχεται ὁ β΄ χορὸς ψάλλων τὸ *Κατευθυνθήτω ἡ προσευχή μου*, ὁ Ἱερεὺς εὐλογεῖ τὸ θυμίαμα καὶ θυμιᾷ τὴν ἁγίαν Τράπεζαν, εἶτα τὰς ἁγίας εἰκόνας καὶ τὸν λαὸν κατὰ τὴν τάξιν· καί, ἐὰν μὲν μέλλῃ ποιῆσαι Εἴσοδον, ἐνδύεται εὐθὺς κατὰ τὸ *Κύριε ἐκέκραξα* καὶ τὸ φελώνιον, εἰδεμὴ θυμιᾷ φέρων τὸ ἐπιτραχήλιον μόνον.

*(Printed Page 44)*

=== LEAF p60 ===

...Meeting of the Lord and the Annunciation)[^61] and at the commemorations of celebrated Saints occurring within the four intermediate weekdays (that is, from Tuesday through Friday), the First Stasis of the Psalter [Psalms 1–3 LXX] is chanted at Vespers, namely the first three psalms. It is not chanted when these Feasts fall on a Saturday or a Monday, because at the Vespers of these two days chanting of the Psalter does not take place.

---

### Concerning Psalm 140
### **"Lord, I have cried"** (*Κύριε ἐκέκραξα*)

**6.** In daily Vespers, the verses of Psalms 140, 141, 129, and 116 LXX—namely, **"Lord, I have cried unto Thee, hear me"** (*Κύριε ἐκέκραξα*),[^62] **"I cried unto the Lord with my voice"** (*Φωνῇ μου πρὸς Κύριον*), **"Out of the depths have I cried unto Thee, O Lord"** (*Ἐκ βαθέων ἐκέκραξά σοι*), and **"Praise the Lord, all ye nations"** (*Αἰνεῖτε τὸν Κύριον*)—are chanted in full in the appointed Tones [Tone 1–8]. In the Vespers of Great Feasts, it is customary for the sake of brevity to omit the verses beginning from **"Set, O Lord, a watch before my mouth"** (*Θοῦ, Κύριε, φυλακήν*) and onward, and for the First Choir to pass directly to the verse appropriate to the appointed number of the subsequent Stichera [10, 8, or 6].

---

### Concerning the First Stichera of Vespers

**7.** At every Saturday Vespers before Sunday, and at every Sunday Matins throughout the entire year, the Resurrection hymns of the Octoechos are chanted without fail—namely: Stichera, Doxastika, Dismissal Troparia, Triadika, Kathismata, Ypakoe, Antiphons, Canons, Kontakia, Ikoses, Exapostilaria, Eothina Gospels, Beatitudes, etc.[^63] These take precedence over the services of the Feasts of the Mother of God and their Apodoses (Leave-takings), as well as over the Apodoses of Feasts of the Lord, post-festal Sundays, and commemorations of Saints when they fall on a Sunday. They are chanted in the following manner:

*(Printed Page 45)*

<!-- END COHORT 1888_violakis_typikon_cohort6 -->

---

<!-- START COHORT 1888_violakis_typikon_cohort7 -->

=== LEAF p61 ===

...τῶν μεθεόρτων Κυριακῶν, καὶ τῶν ἑορταζομένων Ἁγίων, ὅταν συμπίπτωσιν ἐν ἡμέρᾳ Κυριακῇ. Ψάλλονται δὲ οὕτως· ἐν μὲν ταῖς κυρίαις τῶν ἑορτῶν ἡμέραις καὶ ταῖς τῶν Ἀποδόσεων αὐτῶν, πρῶτον ἑσπέρια Στιχηρὰ Ἀναστάσιμα δ΄, εἶτα ς΄ τῆς τυχούσης Θεομητορικῆς ἑορτῆς· ἐν δὲ ταῖς μεθεόρτοις Κυριακαῖς καὶ πάσαις ταῖς τῶν ἑορταζομένων Ἁγίων μνήμαις, Ἀναστάσιμα ς΄ καὶ μεθέορτα ἢ τοῦ Ἁγίου δ΄. Ὅταν δὲ συμπίπτῃ ἐν μεθεόρτῳ Κυριακῇ ἢ ἐν τῇ Ἀποδόσει Δεσποτικῆς ἢ Θεομητορικῆς ἑορτῆς καὶ ἑορταζόμενος Ἅγιος, τότε ψάλλονται Ἀναστάσιμα δ΄, τῆς ἑορτῆς γ΄ καὶ τοῦ Ἁγίου γ΄. Ἐν ταῖς τῶν ἐφεξῆς Ἁγίων ἑορταῖς ἤτοι, τῇ ἑορτῇ τῶν Ἀρχαγγέλων, τοῦ Γενεσίου τοῦ Προδρόμου, τῶν Κορυφαίων Ἀποστόλων Πέτρου καὶ Παύλου, τοῦ Εὐαγγελιστοῦ Ἰωάννου (Σεπτεμβρίου κς΄), τῶν Τριῶν Ἱεραρχῶν (ἂν μὴ συμπίπτῃ ἡ ἑορτὴ αὐτῶν ἔν τινι Κυριακῇ Τριῳδίου), τῶν Ἁγίων Πατέρων τῆς Δ΄ καὶ Ζ΄ Συνόδου, καὶ τῶν Ἁγίων Δημητρίου καὶ Νικολάου, ψάλλονται κατ᾿ ἐξαίρεσιν Ἀναστάσιμα δ΄ καὶ τῶν Ἁγίων ς΄. Ὅταν δὲ ὁ τυχὼν ἐν Κυριακῇ Ἅγιος στερῆται Δοξαστικοῦ, ἐν τῷ Ἑσπερινῷ ψάλλονται Ἀναστάσιμα ζ΄ καὶ τοῦ Ἁγίου γ΄. Καταλιμπάνονται δὲ ἅπαντα τὰ Ἀναστάσιμα ἐν ταῖς ἑξῆς Μ. Δεσποτικαῖς ἑορταῖς, ἤτοι τῇ ἑορτῇ τῆς Χριστοῦ Γεννήσεως, τῶν Θεοφανίων, τῶν Βαΐων, τοῦ Θωμᾶ, τῆς Πεντηκοστῆς, τῆς Μεταμορφώσεως, τῆς Ὑψώσεως τοῦ Σταυροῦ, καὶ τῇ Κυριακῇ πρὸ τῆς Χριστοῦ Γεννήσεως, ὅταν συμπίπτῃ τῇ κδ΄ τοῦ Δεκεμβρίου, ὅτε ὅμως ψάλλεται ἐν τῷ Ὄρθρῳ μετὰ τὸ Θεὸς Κύριος, καὶ ἐν τῇ Λειτουργίᾳ μετὰ τὴν Εἴσοδον, τὸ Ἀπολυτίκιον τοῦ τυχόντος ἤχου, ὡς καὶ τὸ Ἐξαποστειλάριον, καὶ δ΄ τῶν Αἴνων Ἀναστάσιμα.

---

## ΠΕΡΙ ΤΟΥ Α΄ ΕΣΠΕΡΙΟΥ ΔΟΞΑΣΤΙΚΟΥ

**8.** Ἄν συμπέσῃ ἐν ἡμέρᾳ Κυριακῆς Θεομητορικὴ ἑορτή, ἐν τῷ Ἑσπερινῷ Δόξα, Καὶ νῦν ψάλλεται τὸ τῆς ἑορτῆς μόνον. Ἐν ταῖς Ἀποδόσεσιν αὐτῶν (ἐξαιρουμένης τῆς ἀποδόσεως τῆς Κοιμήσεως τῆς Θεοτόκου, ὅτε ψάλλεται Δόξα Καὶ νῦν μόνον θεαρχίῳ νεύματι) καὶ ταῖς τῶν Δεσποτικῶν ἑορτῶν, ἐν ταῖς μεθεόρτοις Κυριακαῖς καὶ ἐν πᾶσι τοῖς ἐν ἡμέρᾳ Κυριακῇ ἑορταζομένοις Ἁγίοις, Δόξα ψάλλεται τὸ τῆς τυχούσης ἑορτῆς, ἢ τοῦ ἑορταζομένου Ἁγίου, Καὶ νῦν τὸ α΄ Θεοτοκίον τοῦ ἤχου. Ὅταν ἡ ἑορτὴ τῆς Ὑπαπαντῆς συμπέσῃ μιᾷ τῶν...

*(Printed Page 46)*

=== LEAF p62 ===

...of Afterfeast Sundays and celebrated Saints, whenever they coincide on a Sunday.[^64] They are chanted as follows: on the principal days of the feasts and on their Apodoses: first 4 evening Resurrection Stichera, then 6 of the occurring Feast of the Mother of God; on Afterfeast Sundays and all commemorations of celebrated Saints: 6 Resurrection Stichera and 4 afterfeast or of the Saint. But whenever a celebrated Saint coincides on an Afterfeast Sunday or on the Apodosis of a Feast of the Lord or of the Mother of God, then 4 Resurrection Stichera, 3 of the feast, and 3 of the Saint are chanted. On the feasts of the following Saints, namely: the Feast of the Archangels [November 8], the Nativity of the Forerunner [June 24], the Prime Apostles Peter and Paul [June 29], the Evangelist John (September 26), the Three Hierarchs [January 30] (unless their feast coincides on a Sunday of the Triodion), the Holy Fathers of the Fourth [July 13–19] and Seventh [October 11–18] Ecumenical Councils, and Saints Demetrius [October 26] and Nicholas [December 6], by exception 4 Resurrection Stichera and 6 of the Saints are chanted. When the Saint occurring on Sunday lacks a Doxastikon, at Vespers 7 Resurrection Stichera and 3 of the Saint are chanted. All the Resurrection hymns are omitted on the following Great Feasts of the Lord, namely: the Feast of the Nativity of Christ, Theophany, Palm Sunday, St. Thomas Sunday, Pentecost, the Transfiguration, the Exaltation of the Holy Cross, and the Sunday before the Nativity of Christ when it coincides on December 24, on which day, however, at Matins after **"God is the Lord"** (*Θεὸς Κύριος*), and at the Divine Liturgy after the Little Entrance, the Dismissal Troparion of the occurring Tone [Tone 1–8] is chanted, as well as the Exaposteilarion, and 4 Resurrection Stichera of the Praises (*Ainoi*).

---

## Concerning the First Evening Doxastikon

**8.** If a Feast of the Mother of God coincides on a Sunday, at Vespers for *Glory...*, *Now and forever...*, only that of the feast is chanted.[^65] On their Apodoses (with the exception of the Apodosis of the Dormition of the Mother of God, when for *Glory...*, *Now and forever...*, only **"By the royal command of God"** [*Θεαρχίῳ νεύματι*] is chanted) and those of Feasts of the Lord, on Afterfeast Sundays, and on all celebrated Saints falling on a Sunday: for *Glory...*, that of the occurring feast or of the celebrated Saint is chanted; for *Now and forever...*, the first Theotokion of the Tone (Dogmatikon) [Tone 1–8]. When the Feast of the Meeting of the Lord coincides on one of the...

*(Printed Page 47)*

=== LEAF p63 ===

...πρώτων τεσσάρων Κυριακῶν τοῦ Τριῳδίου, Δόξα ψάλλεται τοῦ Τριῳδίου, Καὶ νῦν τὸ τῆς ἑορτῆς. Ὅταν ἡ ἑορτὴ τοῦ Εὐαγγελισμοῦ συμπέσῃ τῇ Γ΄ Κυριακῇ τῶν Νηστειῶν, τῷ Σαββάτῳ τοῦ Λαζάρου, ἢ τῇ Κυριακῇ τοῦ Πάσχα, ψάλλεται τὸ τοῦ Τριῳδίου ἢ Πεντηκοσταρίου Δοξαστικὸν πρῶτον, Καὶ νῦν τὸ τῆς ἑορτῆς. Ἄν συμπέσῃ ὅμως τῇ Κυριακῇ τῶν Βαΐων καὶ τῇ Δευτέρᾳ τῆς Διακαινησίμου, Δόξα, Καὶ νῦν λέγεται τὸ τῆς ἑορτῆς μόνον. Ὅταν αἱ ἑορταὶ τῶν ἁγίων Γεωργίου, Ἰωάννου τοῦ Θεολόγου (Μαΐου η΄) καὶ Κωνσταντίνου καὶ Ἑλένης τύχωσιν ἔν τινι Κυριακῇ τοῦ Πεντηκοσταρίου, ἢ ἐν Δεσποτικῇ ἑορτῇ (ἐξαιρουμένης τῆς Κυριακῆς τῆς Πεντηκοστῆς), Δόξα ψάλλεται τῶν Ἁγίων, Καὶ νῦν, τῆς τυχούσης Κυριακῆς ἢ ἑορτῆς. Εἰ ὁ ἐν ἡμέρᾳ Κυριακῇ τυχὼν Ἅγιος στερεῖται Δοξαστικοῦ, Δόξα, Καὶ νῦν ψάλλεται τὸ τοῦ ἤχου Θεοτοκίον· εἰ δέ ἐστι μεγαλoμάρτυς καὶ συμπίπτει ἔν τινι μεθεόρτῳ Κυριακῇ Δεσποτικῆς ἢ Θεομητορικῆς ἑορτῆς, ψάλλεται Δόξα τὸ τοῦ Ἁγίου, Καὶ νῦν, τὸ α΄ Θεοτοκίον τοῦ ἤχου.

---

## ΠΕΡΙ ΤΟΥ ΕΝ ΤΩ ΕΣΠΕΡΙΝΩ ΠΡΟΚΕΙΜΕΝΟΥ

**9.** Ἐν τῷ Ἑσπερινῷ τοῦ Σαββάτου, κἂν τύχωσι Θεομητορικαὶ ἢ Δεσποτικαὶ ἑορταὶ (ἐξαιρουμένων τῶν δύο ἑορτῶν τῆς Χριστοῦ Γεννήσεως καὶ τῶν Θεοφανίων), Προκείμενον ψάλλεται τὸ Ὁ Κύριος ἐβασίλευσεν· ἐξαιροῦνται οἱ ἐν ἡμέρᾳ Σαββάτου, ἤ καὶ ἐν οἱᾳδήποτε ἄλλῃ ἡμέρᾳ συμπίπτοντες μεθέορτοι Ἑσπερινοὶ τῶν Μ. Δεσποτικῶν ἑορτῶν, ἐν οἷς ψάλλονται πάντοτε τὰ Μ. Προκείμενα Τίς θεὸς μέγας, καὶ τὸ Ὁ θεὸς ὑμῶν ἐν τῷ οὐρανῷ καὶ ἐν τῇ γῇ, μετὰ τῶν στίχων αὐτῶν. Ἐν τῷ Ἑσπερινῷ τοῦ Μ. Σαββάτου μετὰ τὴν τοῦ Ἀποστόλου ἀνάγνωσιν ψάλλεται τὸ Προκείμενον Ἀνάστα ὁ Θεός, μετὰ τῶν ὡρισμένων στίχων.

---

## ΠΕΡΙ ΤΩΝ ΕΝ ΤΩ ΕΣΠΕΡΙΝΩ ΑΝΑΓΝΩΣΜΑΤΩΝ

**10.** Ἐν τῷ Ἑσπερινῷ τοῦ Σαββάτου ἀναγινώσκονται Προφητεῖαι, ὅταν συμπίπτωσι τῇ Κυριακῇ Δεσποτικαὶ καὶ Θεομητορικαὶ ἑορταί,...

*(Printed Page 48)*

=== LEAF p64 ===

...first four Sundays of the Triodion: for *Glory...*, that of the Triodion is chanted; for *Now and forever...*, that of the feast. When the Feast of the Annunciation coincides on the Third Sunday of Great Lent, on Lazarus Saturday, or on Easter Sunday: the Doxastikon of the Triodion or Pentecostarion is chanted first; for *Now and forever...*, that of the feast. If, however, it coincides on Palm Sunday or on Bright Monday, for *Glory...*, *Now and forever...*, only that of the feast is said. When the feasts of Saints George [April 23], John the Theologian (May 8), and Constantine and Helen [May 21] occur on any Sunday of the Pentecostarion, or on a Feast of the Lord (with the exception of Pentecost Sunday): for *Glory...*, that of the Saints is chanted; for *Now and forever...*, that of the occurring Sunday or feast. If the Saint occurring on Sunday lacks a Doxastikon, for *Glory...*, *Now and forever...*, the Theotokion of the Tone is chanted. But if he is a great martyr and coincides on an Afterfeast Sunday of a Feast of the Lord or of the Mother of God: for *Glory...*, that of the Saint is chanted; for *Now and forever...*, the first Theotokion of the Tone (Dogmatikon) [Tone 1–8].

---

## Concerning the Prokimenon of Vespers

**9.** At Saturday Vespers, even if Feasts of the Mother of God or of the Lord occur (with the exception of the two feasts of the Nativity of Christ and Theophany), the Prokimenon chanted is **"The Lord is King, He is robed in majesty"** (*Ὁ Κύριος ἐβασίλευσεν*, Psalm 92:1 LXX);[^66] except for the afterfeast Vespers of Great Feasts of the Lord falling on a Saturday, or on any other day of the week, at which are always chanted the Great Prokimena: **"Who is a god as great as our God? Thou art the God Who workest wonders"** (*Τίς θεὸς μέγας ὡς ὁ θεὸς ἡμῶν; σὺ εἶ ὁ θεὸς ὁ ποιῶν θαυμάσια*, Psalm 76:14 LXX) and **"Our God is in heaven and on earth; all things whatsoever He willed, He hath done"** (*Ὁ δὲ θεὸς ἡμῶν ἐν τῷ οὐρανῷ καὶ ἐν τῇ γῇ, πάντα ὅσα ἠθέλησεν ἐποίησεν*, Psalm 113:11 LXX), with their verses. At the Vespers of Holy Saturday, after the reading of the Apostle (Epistle), the Prokimenon **"Arise, O God, judge the earth"** (*Ἀνάστα ὁ Θεός, κρῖνον τὴν γῆν*, Psalm 81:8 LXX) is chanted with its appointed verses.

---

## Concerning the Readings in Vespers

**10.** At Saturday Vespers, Old Testament Prophecies are read whenever Feasts of the Lord and of the Mother of God coincide on Sunday,...

*(Printed Page 49)*

=== LEAF p65 ===

...ἢ μνῆμαι ἑορταζομένων Ἁγίων· καὶ πρὸ τῆς ἀναγνώσεως αὐτῶν ὁ Διάκονος ἐπιφωνεῖ τὸ Σοφία πρόσχωμεν· τὰ Ἀποστολικὰ δὲ Ἀναγνώσματα, ἅτινα ἀναγινώσκονται ἐν ταῖς μνήμαις τῶν Ἀποστόλων Πέτρου καὶ Παύλου, Ἰωάννου τοῦ Θεολόγου, Ἰακώβου τοῦ Ἀδελφοθέου, καὶ Ἀνδρέου τοῦ Πρωτοκλήτου, ἀναγινώσκονται συνήθως κατὰ τὴν ἀπαγγελίαν τοῦ ἐν τῇ Λειτουργίᾳ ἀναγινωσκομένου Ἀποστόλου καὶ ὁ Διάκονος ἐκφωνεῖ τὸ Πρόσχωμεν, σοφία, πρόσχωμεν.

---

## ΠΕΡΙ ΑΡΤΟΚΛΑΣΙΑΣ

**11.** Ἄν ὑπάρχῃ Ἀρτοκλασία, μετὰ τὴν ἐκφώνησιν Εἴη τὸ κράτος τῆς βασιλείας σου, ψάλλεται ὑπὸ τοῦ β΄ χοροῦ ἓν κατ᾿ ἐκλογὴν Στιχηρὸν ἐκ τῆς Λιτῆς, καὶ ἐν τούτῳ τῷ μεταξὺ ἐξέρχεται ἀπὸ τοῦ βορείου κλίτους ὁ Διάκονος καὶ ὁ Ἱερεὺς καὶ ἔρχονται φέροντες εἰς χεῖρας λαμπάδας, καὶ ἵστανται ἐν τῷ μέσῳ τοῦ ναοῦ, ὅπου εἰσὶ προτεθειμένοι οἱ ἄρτοι, οἶνος καὶ ἔλαιον· ὁ Διάκονος τότε λέγει τὸ Ἐλέησον ἡμᾶς ὁ Θεός... Ἔτι δεόμεθα ὑπὲρ τοῦ διαφυλαχθῆναι τὴν ἁγίαν ἐκκλησίαν ταύτην, τὴν πόλιν (ἢ χώραν) ταύτην κτλ., οἱ χοροὶ ψάλλουσιν εὐλαβῶς καὶ πραείᾳ τῇ φωνῇ τὸ Κύριε ἐλέησον ὡς ἡ τάξις· ὁ Ἱερεὺς Ἐπάκουσον ἡμῶν ὁ Θεός... Μ. Προκείμενα ψάλλονται ὡσαύτως καὶ ἐν τοῖς Ἑσπερινοῖς τῆς Διακαινησίμου ἑβδομάδος, ὡς διατυποῦνται ἐν τῷ Πεντηκοσταρίῳ. Εἰρήνη πᾶσι· ὁ Χορὸς Καὶ τῷ πνεύματί σου· ὁ Διάκονος Τὰς κεφαλὰς ἡμῶν... ὁ χορὸς Σοὶ Κύριε· ὁ Ἱερεὺς (ἢ ὁ Ἀρχιερεὺς) Δέσποτα Πολυέλεε... εἶτα ὁ Ἱερεὺς θυμιᾷ σταυροειδῶς στρεφόμενος περὶ τοὺς ἄρτους καὶ ψάλλων τὸ Θεοτόκε Παρθένε... Ἐν πάσαις ἀνεξαιρέτως ταῖς Δεσποτικαῖς καὶ Θεομητορικαῖς ἑορταῖς ὡς καὶ ἐν ταῖς τῶν Ἁγίων μνήμαις, ἐὰν γίνηται Ἀρτοκλασία, ψάλλεται πάντοτε τὸ Θεοτόκε Παρθένε. Ἀλλ᾿ ἐν τῇ Διακαινησίμῳ ἑβδομάδι, ἀντὶ τοῦ Θεοτόκε Παρθένε ψάλλεται τὸ Χριστὸς ἀνέστη. Τούτου πληρωθέντος, λέγει ὁ Διάκονος Τοῦ Κυρίου δεηθῶμεν· ὁ χορὸς Κύριε ἐλέησον· ὁ Ἱερεὺς εὐλογεῖ τοὺς ἄρτους, λέγων τὴν εὐχὴν Δέσποτα Κύριε Ἰησοῦ Χριστὲ ὁ Θεὸς ἡμῶν... εἶτα ψάλλεται τρὶς τὸ Πλούσιοι ἐπτώχευσαν, καὶ μετ᾿ αὐτὸ τὰ Ἀπόστιχα.

---

## ΠΕΡΙ ΤΩΝ ΕΣΠΕΡΙΩΝ ΑΠΟΣΤΙΧΩΝ

**12.** Τὰ ἀναστάσιμα Ἀπόστιχα κατὰ τὸν Ἑσπερινὸν τοῦ Σαββάτου...

*(Printed Page 50)*

=== LEAF p66 ===

...or commemorations of celebrated Saints;[^67] and before their reading, the Deacon proclaims: **"Wisdom! Let us be attentive!"** (*Σοφία, πρόσχωμεν!*). The Apostolic Epistle readings that are read on the commemorations of the Apostles Peter and Paul [June 29], John the Theologian [May 8], James the Brother of the Lord [October 23], and Andrew the First-Called [November 30] are customarily read according to the manner of delivery of the Epistle (*Apostolos*) read at the Divine Liturgy, and the Deacon intones: **"Let us be attentive! Wisdom! Let us be attentive!"** (*Πρόσχωμεν, σοφία, πρόσχωμεν!*).

---

## Concerning the Artoklasia

**11.** If an Artoklasia (Blessing of the Loaves) is held,[^68] after the exclamation **"Blessed and glorified be the majesty of Thy kingdom..."** (*Εἴη τὸ κράτος τῆς βασιλείας σου...*), a chosen Sticheron from the Litiya is chanted by the Second Choir; meanwhile, the Deacon and the Priest exit through the north door [of the iconostasis] holding candles in their hands, and stand in the middle of the temple before the Tetrapod, where the loaves, wheat, wine, and oil have been prepared. The Deacon then intones the litany: **"Have mercy on us, O God, according to Thy great mercy..."** (*Ἐλέησον ἡμᾶς ὁ Θεός...*), **"Again we pray for the preservation of this holy temple, this city (or village)..."** (*Ἔτι δεόμεθα ὑπὲρ τοῦ διαφυλαχθῆναι τὴν ἁγίαν ἐκκλησίαν ταύτην...*), etc. The choirs chant **"Lord, have mercy"** reverently and in a subdued voice as the rubrics appoint. The Priest intones: **"Hear us, O God our Savior, the hope of all the ends of the earth..."** (*Ἐπάκουσον ἡμῶν ὁ Θεός...*). (Great Prokimena are likewise chanted at the Vespers of Bright Week, as set forth in the Pentecostarion). The Priest: **"Peace be to all."** Choir: **"And to thy spirit."** Deacon: **"Let us bow our heads unto the Lord."** Choir: **"To Thee, O Lord."** The Priest (or the Hierarch): **"O Master, abundant in mercy..."** (*Δέσποτα Πολυέλεε...*). Then the Priest censes crosswise, circling the Tetrapod with the loaves and chanting: **"O Mother of God and Virgin, rejoice, Mary full of grace, the Lord is with thee; blessed art thou among women, and blessed is the fruit of thy womb, for thou hast borne the Savior of our souls"** (*Θεοτόκε Παρθένε...*). On all Feasts of the Lord and of the Mother of God without exception, as well as on commemorations of Saints, if an Artoklasia is held, **"O Mother of God and Virgin"** is always chanted. But throughout Bright Week, instead of **"O Mother of God and Virgin,"** **"Christ is risen from the dead"** (*Χριστὸς ἀνέστη*) is chanted. This being completed, the Deacon says: **"Let us pray to the Lord."** Choir: **"Lord, have mercy."** The Priest blesses the loaves, reciting the prayer: **"O Lord Jesus Christ our God, Who didst bless the five loaves in the wilderness and therewith feed five thousand..."** (*Δέσποτα Κύριε Ἰησοῦ Χριστὲ ὁ Θεὸς ἡμῶν...*). Then **"The rich have become poor and gone hungry, but they that seek the Lord shall not want any good thing"** (*Πλούσιοι ἐπτώχευσαν*, Psalm 33:11 LXX) is chanted thrice, and after it, the Aposticha.

---

## Concerning the Vespers Aposticha

**12.** The Resurrection Aposticha at Saturday Vespers are never...

*(Printed Page 51)*

=== LEAF p67 ===

...οὐδέποτε καταλιμπάνονται κἂν τύχωσι Θεομητορικαὶ ἑορταί, προεόρτιοι ἢ μεθέορτοι Κυριακαὶ καὶ Ἀποδόσεις Δεσποτικῶν ἢ Θεομητορικῶν ἑορτῶν, ἢ μνῆμαι ἑορταζομένων Ἁγίων. Ἐν τῷ Ἑσπερινῷ τοῦ Σαββάτου τῶν τεσσάρων μετὰ τὴν Κυριακὴν τοῦ Θωμᾶ Κυριακῶν ψάλλεται ἐν πρώτοις τὸ τοῦ ἤχου πρῶτον Ἀπόστιχον (ὅπερ ἄρχεται ἀπὸ τοῦ α΄ χοροῦ), εἶτα ψάλλονται τὰ δ΄ Στιχηρὰ Πάσχα ἱερὸν μετὰ τῶν στίχων αὐτῶν.

---

## ΠΕΡΙ ΤΟΥ ΔΟΞΑΣΤΙΚΟΥ ΤΩΝ ΑΠΟΣΤΙΧΩΝ

**13.** Ἐν ταῖς συμπιπτούσαις ἐν ἡμέρᾳ Κυριακῇ Θεομητορικαῖς ἑορταῖς, ταῖς μεθεόρτοις Κυριακαῖς αὐτῶν τε καὶ τῶν Δεσποτικῶν ἑορτῶν καὶ τῶν προεορτίων αὐτῶν ὡς καὶ τῶν Ἀποδόσεων αὐτῶν, τῇ Γ΄ Κυριακῇ τῶν Νηστειῶν, τῇ Κυριακῇ τῶν Μυροφόρων, τῇ ἑορτῇ τῶν Θεοπατόρων Ἰωακεὶμ καὶ Ἄννης καὶ τῇ τῶν Ταξιαρχῶν (ὅταν συμπίπτωσιν ἐν Κυριακῇ) Δοξαστικὸν τῶν Ἀποστίχων ψάλλεται μόνον τὸ τῆς τυχούσης ἑορτῆς ἄνευ θεοτοκίου. Ὅταν ἡ ἑορτὴ τῆς Ὑπαπαντῆς καὶ ἡ Ἀπόδοσις αὐτῆς συμπίπτῃ ἐν μιᾷ τῶν τεσσάρων πρώτων Κυριακῶν τοῦ Τριῳδίου, ψάλλεται Δόξα τὸ τῶν Ἀποστίχων τοῦ Τριῳδίου, Καὶ νῦν τὸ τῆς ἑορτῆς. Καὶ ἡ τοῦ Εὐαγγελισμοῦ ἑορτὴ ὅταν συμπίπτῃ τῇ Γ΄ Κυριακῇ τῶν Νηστειῶν ἢ τῇ τῶν Βαΐων, ψάλλονται τὰ τοῦ Τριῳδίου Ἀπόστιχα, Δοξαστικά, Καὶ νῦν τὸ τῆς ἑορτῆς· ἐὰν δὲ συμπέσῃ τῷ Πάσχα, τότε ἐν τῷ Ἑσπερινῷ λέγεται Δόξα τῆς ἑορτῆς, Καὶ νῦν Ἀναστάσεως ἡμέρα. Ἐν ταῖς ἐν ἡμέρᾳ Κυριακῆς συμπιπτούσαις Ἁγίου μνήμαις, Δόξα τῶν Ἀποστίχων ψάλλεται τὸ τοῦ Ἁγίου, Καὶ νῦν θεοτοκίον ἐκ τῆς Ὀκτωήχου σύμφωνον τῷ ἤχῳ τοῦ Δοξαστικοῦ. Ὅταν δὲ αἱ τῶν ἑορταζομένων Ἁγίων μνῆμαι συμπίπτωσιν ἐν μεθεόρτῳ Κυριακῇ, ἢ Ἀποδόσει Δεσποτικῆς τινος ἢ Θεομητορικῆς ἑορτῆς, Δόξα Ἀπόστιχον ψάλλεται τὸ τοῦ Ἁγίου, Καὶ νῦν τὸ τῆς τυχούσης ἑορτῆς. Τοῦ ἐν ἡμέρᾳ Κυριακῆς τυχόντος Ἁγίου μὴ ἔχοντος εἰς τὰ Ἀπόστιχα Δοξαστικόν, Δόξα Καὶ νῦν ψάλλεται τὸ τῶν Ἀποστίχων θεοτοκίον τοῦ ἤχου.

  Σημειωτέον δὲ ὅτι ἐν ταῖς μετὰ τὸ Πάσχα ἐντὸς τῶν ἓξ ἡμερῶν τῆς ἑβδομάδος (πλὴν δηλ. τῆς Κυριακῆς) μέχρι τῆς Ἀναλήψεως συμπιπτούσαις μνήμαις τῶν ἁγίων Γεωργίου, Ἰωάννου τοῦ Θεολόγου, Ἀθανασίου τοῦ Μεγάλου καὶ Κωνσταντίνου καὶ Ἑλένης, Ἀπόστιχα Δοξαστικὰ ψάλλονται τὰ τῶν Ἁγίων, Καὶ...

*(Printed Page 52)*

=== LEAF p68 ===

...omitted, even if Feasts of the Mother of God, Forefeast or Afterfeast Sundays, or Apodoses of Feasts of the Lord or of the Mother of God, or commemorations of celebrated Saints occur.[^69] At Saturday Vespers of the four Sundays following St. Thomas Sunday [Myrrh-Bearers, Paralytic, Samaritan Woman, Blind Man], first the first Resurrection Apostichon of the Tone is chanted (which begins from the First Choir), then the 4 Paschal Stichera **"A sacred Pascha"** (*Πάσχα ἱερὸν*) with their verses are chanted.

---

## Concerning the Doxastikon of the Aposticha

**13.** On Feasts of the Mother of God coinciding on Sunday, on their Afterfeast Sundays, on Feasts of the Lord and their Forefeasts and their Apodoses, on the Third Sunday of Great Lent [Veneration of the Precious Cross], on the Sunday of the Myrrh-Bearers, on the feast of the Holy Ancestors of God Joachim and Anna [September 9], and on the Archangels [November 8 (when it falls on Sunday)], only the Doxastikon of the Aposticha of the occurring feast is chanted, without a Theotokion.[^70] When the Feast of the Meeting of the Lord or its Apodosis coincides on one of the first four Sundays of the Triodion: for *Glory...*, that of the Aposticha of the Triodion is chanted; for *Now and forever...*, that of the feast. When the Feast of the Annunciation coincides on the Third Sunday of Great Lent or on Palm Sunday: the Aposticha and Doxastikon of the Triodion are chanted; for *Now and forever...*, that of the feast. If it coincides on Pascha Sunday, then at Vespers: *Glory...*, of the feast; *Now and forever...*, **"It is the day of Resurrection"** (*Ἀναστάσεως ἡμέρα*). On commemorations of Saints occurring on a Sunday: for *Glory...* of the Aposticha, that of the Saint is chanted; for *Now and forever...*, the Theotokion from the Octoechos in the same Tone [Tone 1–8] as the Doxastikon of the Saint. But when the commemorations of celebrated Saints coincide on an Afterfeast Sunday or on the Apodosis of a Feast of the Lord or of the Mother of God: for *Glory...* of the Aposticha, that of the Saint is chanted; for *Now and forever...*, that of the occurring feast. If the Saint occurring on Sunday has no Doxastikon at the Aposticha, for *Glory...*, *Now and forever...*, the Theotokion of the Aposticha of the Tone is chanted.

  It is to be noted that on the days after Pascha throughout the six days of the week (that is, excluding Sunday) until the Ascension, when the commemorations of Saints George [April 23], John the Theologian [May 8], Athanasius the Great [May 2], and Constantine and Helen [May 21] coincide: for the Aposticha Doxastika, those of the Saints are chanted; and for *Now and forever...*,...

*(Printed Page 53)*

=== LEAF p69 ===

...νῦν δὲ ἐκ τοῦ Πεντηκοσταρίου (τὸ Δοξαστικὸν) τῆς προλαβούσης Κυριακῆς ἢ ἑορτῆς, καὶ οὐχὶ τὸ Ἀναστάσεως ἡμέρα, τὸ ὁποῖον μόνον ἐν τῷ Ἑσπερινῷ τοῦ Σαββάτου καὶ τῷ τῶν Κυριακῶν Ὄρθρῳ δεῖ ψάλλεσθαι.

---

## ΠΕΡΙ ΤΟΥ ΝΥΝ ΑΠΟΛΥΕΙΣ ΚΑΙ ΤΟΥ ΤΡΙΣΑΓΙΟΥ

**14.** Καθ᾿ ἑκάστην ἡμέραν ἐν τῷ Ἑσπερινῷ πρὸ τῆς Ἀπολύσεως λέγεται τὸ Νῦν ἀπολύεις μετὰ τοῦ Τρισαγίου, ἐν δὲ τῇ Διακαινησίμῳ ἑβδομάδι καὶ ἐν τῷ Ἑσπερινῷ τῆς Ἀποδόσεως τοῦ Πάσχα, μετὰ τό Ἀναστάσεως ἡμέρα, ἀντὶ τοῦ Τρισαγίου, ψάλλεται τὸ Χριστὸς ἀνέστη τρὶς κατὰ τὸ σύνηθες αὐτοῦ μέλος καὶ οὐχὶ κατὰ τὸ μέλος τοῦ Ἀναστάσεως ἡμέρα, καὶ εὐθὺς ἕπεται ἡ Ἀπόλυσις.

---

## ΠΕΡΙ ΤΩΝ ΑΠΟΛΥΤΙΚΙΩΝ

**15.** Ἐν τῷ τοῦ Σαββάτου Ἑσπερινῷ ψάλλονται τὰ Ἀναστάσιμα τῆς Ὀκτωήχου Ἀπολυτίκια πρὸ τῶν Ἀπολυτικίων τῶν ἐν ἡμέρᾳ Κυριακῆς συμπιπτουσῶν Θεομητορικῶν ἑορτῶν, τῶν μεθεόρτων Κυριακῶν καὶ τῶν Ἀποδόσεων Δεσποτικῶν τε καὶ Θεομητορικῶν ἑορτῶν. Ψάλλονται δὲ ἐν μὲν ταῖς κυρίαις (θεομητορικαῖς) ἑορταῖς καὶ ἐν ταῖς Ἀποδόσεσι τὸ Ἀναστάσιμον ἅπαξ καὶ τὸ τῆς ἑορτῆς δίς. Ἐν δὲ ταῖς μεθεόρτοις Κυριακαῖς καὶ τὸ Ἀναστάσιμον καὶ τὸ τῆς ἑορτῆς ἅπαξ· ἀλλ᾿ ἐν τῷ Ὄρθρῳ τὸ Ἀναστάσιμον λέγεται δὶς καὶ τὸ τῆς ἑορτῆς ἅπαξ. Τῆς ἑορτῆς τοῦ Εὐαγγελισμοῦ συμπιπτούσης τῇ Γ΄ Κυριακῇ τῶν Νηστειῶν, ψάλλεται πρῶτον τὸ Ἀναστάσιμον, δεύτερον τὸ τοῦ Σταυροῦ, καὶ τρίτον τὸ τῆς ἑορτῆς· τῷ Σαββάτῳ τοῦ Λαζάρου, τὸ τῆς ἑορτῆς δίς, καὶ τὸ τοῦ Λαζάρου ἅπαξ· τῇ Κυριακῇ τῶν Βαΐων, τὸ τῆς ἑορτῆς, τὸ τοῦ Λαζάρου, καὶ αὖθις τὸ τῆς ἑορτῆς. Ἐν ταῖς προεόρτοις Κυριακαῖς τῶν Δεσποτικῶν καὶ Θεομητορικῶν ἑορτῶν, ψάλλεται ἀφ᾿ ἑσπέρας μὲν τὸ Ἀναστάσιμον καὶ τὸ Προεόρτιον ἀνὰ μίαν· τὸ πρωῒ δὲ εἰς τὸ Θεὸς Κύριος, διπλασιάζεται τὸ πρῶτον. Ἑορταζομένου Ἁγίου μνήμης τυχούσης ἐν μεθεόρτῳ Κυριακῇ ἢ ἀποδόσει Δεσποτικῆς ἢ Θεομητορικῆς ἑορτῆς, ψάλλεται πρῶτον τὸ Ἀναστάσιμον, εἶτα τὸ τοῦ Ἁγίου καὶ τελευταῖον τὸ τῆς τυχούσης ἑορτῆς· τοῦ δὲ μὴ ἔχοντος Δοξαστικὸν Ἁγίου ἐν τῷ Ἑσπερινῷ τοῦ...

*(Printed Page 54)*

=== LEAF p70 ===

...that from the Pentecostarion (the Doxastikon) of the preceding Sunday or feast, and not **"It is the day of Resurrection"** (*Ἀναστάσεως ἡμέρα*), which is appointed to be chanted only at Saturday Vespers and Sunday Matins.

---

## Concerning "Now Let Your Servant Depart" and the Trisagion

**14.** Daily at Vespers before the Dismissal, **"Now let Your servant depart in peace, O Master, according to Thy word"** (*Νῦν ἀπολύεις*, Luke 2:29–32) is said with the Trisagion Prayers.[^71] But throughout Bright Week and at the Vespers of the Apodosis of Pascha, after **"It is the day of Resurrection,"** instead of the Trisagion Prayers, **"Christ is risen from the dead"** (*Χριστὸς ἀνέστη*) is chanted thrice according to its customary melody (and not according to the melody of **"It is the day of Resurrection"**), and the Dismissal immediately follows.

---

## Concerning the Dismissal Troparia

**15.** At Saturday Vespers, the Resurrection Dismissal Troparia of the Octoechos are chanted before the Dismissal Troparia of Feasts of the Mother of God coinciding on Sunday, of Afterfeast Sundays, and of the Apodoses of Feasts of the Lord and of the Mother of God.[^72] They are chanted as follows: on the principal Feasts of the Mother of God and on their Apodoses: the Resurrection Troparion once, and that of the feast twice. On Afterfeast Sundays: both the Resurrection Troparion and that of the feast once; but at Matins, the Resurrection Troparion is said twice and that of the feast once. If the Feast of the Annunciation coincides on the Third Sunday of Great Lent, there are chanted: first the Resurrection Troparion, second that of the Cross, and third that of the feast; on Lazarus Saturday: that of the feast twice, and that of Lazarus once; on Palm Sunday: that of the feast, that of Lazarus, and again that of the feast. On Forefeast Sundays of Feasts of the Lord and of the Mother of God, at evening the Resurrection Troparion and the Forefeast Troparion are chanted once each; but in the morning at **"God is the Lord,"** the first is doubled. When the commemoration of a celebrated Saint occurs on an Afterfeast Sunday or on the Apodosis of a Feast of the Lord or of the Mother of God, there are chanted: first the Resurrection Troparion, then that of the Saint, and last that of the occurring feast. When a Saint lacking a Doxastikon occurs at Saturday Vespers...

*(Printed Page 55)*

<!-- END COHORT 1888_violakis_typikon_cohort7 -->

---

<!-- START COHORT 1888_violakis_typikon_cohort8 -->

=== LEAF p71 ===

...Σαββάτου Ἀπολυτίκιον οὐ ψάλλεται, εἰμὴ μόνον τὸ Ἀναστάσιμον μετὰ τοῦ Θεοτοκίου αὐτοῦ, ἀλλ᾿ οὐδὲ καὶ ἐν τῷ Ὄρθρῳ ἢ τῇ Λειτουργίᾳ.

---

## ΠΕΡΙ ΤΗΣ ΑΠΟΛΥΣΕΩΣ ΕΝ ΓΕΝΕΙ

**16.** Ἡ ἐν τῷ Ἑσπερινῷ τοῦ Σαββάτου ὑπὸ τοῦ Ἱερέως λεγομένη Ἀπόλυσίς ἐστιν αὕτη· ὁ Διάκονος *Σοφία*, ὁ Ἱερεὺς *Ὁ ὢν εὐλογητὸς ὁ Θεὸς ἡμῶν, πάντοτε νῦν καὶ ἀεὶ καὶ εἰς τοὺς αἰῶνας τῶν αἰώνων*· ὁ Χορὸς *Ἀμήν*· ὁ προϊστάμενος *Στερεώσαι Κύριος ὁ Θεὸς τὴν ἁγίαν πίστιν τῶν εὐσεβῶν καὶ ὀρθοδόξων χριστιανῶν σὺν τῇ ἁγίᾳ αὐτοῦ ταύτῃ ἐκκλησίᾳ καὶ τῇ πόλει (ἤ τῇ χώρᾳ) ταύτῃ εἰς αἰῶνας αἰώνων*· ὁ Χορὸς *Ἀμήν* (Ἀρτοκλασίας ὅμως ὑπαρχούσης, ὁ Διάκονος ἀντὶ τοῦ *Σοφία*, λέγει *τοῦ Κυρίου δεηθῶμεν*· ὁ χορὸς *Κύριε ἐλέησον, Κύριε ἐλέησον, Κύριε ἐλέησον*· ὁ Ἀρχιερεὺς ἢ ὁ Ἱερεὺς *Εὐλογία Κυρίου καὶ ἔλεος αὐτοῦ ἔλθοι ἐφ᾽ ἡμᾶς τῇ αὐτοῦ χάριτι καὶ φιλανθρωπίᾳ πάντοτε νῦν καὶ ἀεὶ καὶ εἰς τοὺς αἰῶνας τῶν αἰώνων*· ὁ χορὸς *Ἀμήν*) καὶ εὐθὺς ὁ Ἱερεὺς τὴν Ἀπόλυσιν. *Δόξα σοι ὁ Θεὸς ἡ ἐλπὶς ἡμῶν δόξα σοι. Ὁ ἀναστὰς ἐκ νεκρῶν Χριστὸς ὁ ἀληθινὸς Θεὸς ἡμῶν, ταῖς πρεσβείαις τῆς παναχράντου αὐτοῦ Μητρός, δυνάμει τοῦ τιμίου καὶ ζωοποιοῦ Σταυροῦ, προστασίαις τῶν τιμίων ἐπουρανίων Δυνάμεων Ἀσωμάτων, τοῦ τιμίου ἐνδόξου Προφήτου Προδρόμου καὶ βαπτιστοῦ Ἰωάννου, τῶν ἁγίων ἐνδόξων καὶ πανευφήμων Ἀποστόλων, τῶν ἁγίων ἐνδόξων καὶ καλλινίκων μαρτύρων, τῶν ὁσίων καὶ θεοφόρων Πατέρων ἡμῶν, τῶν ἁγίων καὶ δικαίων θεοπατόρων Ἰωακεὶμ καὶ Ἄννης* [εἶτα τοῦ Ἁγίου τοῦ Ναοῦ καὶ τοῦ Ἁγίου τῆς ἡμέρας, ἐὰν ἔχῃ Δοξαστικόν], *καὶ πάντων τῶν ἁγίων, ἐλεήσαι καὶ σώσαι ἡμᾶς ὡς ἀγαθὸς καὶ φιλάνθρωπος.*⁶ Εἶτα τὸ *Δι᾽ εὐχῶν τῶν ἁγίων πατέρων ἡμῶν, Κύριε Ἰησοῦ Χριστὲ ὁ Θεός, ἐλέησον ἡμᾶς. Ἀμήν*. Αὕτη λέγεται καὶ τῇ Κυριακῇ ἔν τε τῷ Ὄρθρῳ καὶ ἐν τῇ Λειτουργίᾳ (ἀλλ᾽ ἐν τῇ τῆς Λειτουργίας προστίθεται καὶ τὸ ὄνομα τοῦ Ἁγίου τῆς Λειτουργίας πρὸ τοῦ Ἁγίου τῆς ἡμέρας)· ἡ αὐτὴ καὶ τῇ Κυριακῇ τοῦ Πάσχα. Ἑκάστη δὲ τῶν Δεσποτικῶν ἑορτῶν ἔχει ἰδιαίτερον χαρακτηριστικὸν ἐν τῇ Ἀπολύσει, ὡς γνωστὸν τοῖς...

*(Printed Page 56)*

=== LEAF p72 ===

...does not have a Doxastikon at Saturday Vespers, his Dismissal Troparion is not chanted, neither at Vespers, nor at Matins, nor at the Divine Liturgy; only the Resurrection Dismissal Troparion with its Theotokion is chanted.

---

## Concerning the Dismissal in General

**16.** The Dismissal of Saturday Vespers is as follows: The Deacon: **"Wisdom!"** (*Σοφία*). The Priest: **"Blessed is He Who is, Christ our God, always, now and forever, and unto the ages of ages"** (*Ὁ ὢν εὐλογητὸς Χριστὸς ὁ Θεὸς ἡμῶν...*). The Choir: **"Amen."** (*Ἀμήν*). The Superior [or Senior Priest]: **"Establish, O Lord God, the holy Orthodox faith of pious and Orthodox Christians, together with His holy Church and this city (or country) unto the ages of ages"** (*Στερεώσαι Κύριος ὁ Θεὸς τὴν ἁγίαν πίστιν...*). The Choir: **"Amen."** (*Ἀμήν*). (If, however, an Artoklasia is celebrated, the Deacon says **"Let us pray to the Lord"** [*Τοῦ Κυρίου δεηθῶμεν*] instead of **"Wisdom!"**; the Choir: **"Lord, have mercy; Lord, have mercy; Lord, have mercy"** [*Κύριε ἐλέησον, τρίς*]; the Hierarch or Priest: **"The blessing of the Lord and His mercy come upon you through His divine grace and love for mankind always, now and forever, and unto the ages of ages"** [*Εὐλογία Κυρίου καὶ ἔλεος αὐτοῦ...*]; the Choir: **"Amen."** [*Ἀμήν*].) And immediately the Priest pronounces the Dismissal: **"Glory to Thee, O Christ our God and our hope, glory to Thee"** (*Δόξα σοι, Χριστὲ ὁ Θεός, ἡ ἐλπὶς ἡμῶν, δόξα σοι*). **"May He Who rose from the dead, Christ our true God, through the intercessions of His all-pure and all-immaculate Mother, by the power of the precious and life-giving Cross, through the protection of the honorable heavenly Bodiless Powers, the supplications of the honorable and glorious Prophet, Forerunner, and Baptist John, the holy, glorious, and all-praised Apostles, the holy, glorious, and victorious Martyrs, our venerable and God-bearing Fathers, the holy and righteous Ancestors of God Joachim and Anna [then the patron Saint of the Temple, and the Saint of the day, if he have a Doxastikon], and of all the saints, have mercy on us and save us, for He is good and the Lover of mankind."**[^73] Then: **"Through the prayers of our holy fathers, O Lord Jesus Christ our God, have mercy on us. Amen."** (*Δι᾽ εὐχῶν τῶν ἁγίων πατέρων ἡμῶν...*). This Dismissal is likewise said on Sunday both at Matins and at the Divine Liturgy (but at the Divine Liturgy, the name of the author of the Liturgy is added before the commemoration of the Saint of the day); this same Dismissal is said on Easter Sunday. Each of the Feasts of the Lord has a specific characteristic phrase in the Dismissal, as is familiar to the priests; but on their Afterfeast Sundays and on their Apodoses, if any...

*(Printed Page 57)*

=== LEAF p73 ===

...ἱερεῦσιν· ἀλλ᾿ ἐν ταῖς μεθεόρτοις αὐτῶν Κυριακαῖς καὶ ἐν ταῖς Ἀποδόσεσιν, ἄν συμπέσῃ Κυριακή, ὅτε καὶ προηγοῦνται τὰ Ἀναστάσιμα, ἔν τε τῷ Ἑσπερινῷ τοῦ Σαββάτου καὶ ἐν τῷ Ὄρθρῳ τῆς Κυριακῆς καὶ ἐν τῇ Λειτουργίᾳ, ἡ Ἀπόλυσις φέρει τὰ χαρακτηριστικὰ καὶ τῆς Κυριακῆς καὶ τῆς τυχούσης ἑορτῆς· ὡς *Δόξα σοι ὁ Θεὸς ἡ ἐλπὶς ἡμῶν δόξα σοι· ὁ ἐν σπηλαίῳ γεννηθεὶς καὶ ἐν φάτνῃ ἀνακλιθεὶς* (ἤ *Ὁ ἐν Ἰορδάνῃ ὑπὸ Ἰωάννου βαπτισθῆναι καταδεξάμενος διὰ τὴν ἡμῶν σωτηρίαν*) *καὶ ἀναστὰς ἐκ νεκρῶν Χριστὸς ὁ ἀληθινὸς Θεὸς ἡμῶν κτλ.* Ἐν τῇ ἑορτῇ τῆς Ὑψώσεως τοῦ Σταυροῦ, λέγεται ἡ ἀναστάσιμος Ἀπόλυσις οἵᾳ ἄν ἡμέρᾳ τύχοι ἡ ἑορτή, καθότι καὶ ἐν τῷ Ὄρθρῳ λέγομεν τὸ *Ἀνάστασιν Χριστοῦ*. Σημειωτέον δὲ ὅτι μόνον αἱ δύο Θεομητορικαὶ ἑορταί, ἡ τοῦ Εὐαγγελισμοῦ καὶ ἡ τῆς Ὑπαπαντῆς, ἔχουσιν ἰδιαίτερα χαρακτηριστικὰ ἐν τῇ Ἀπολύσει, ἅτινα, ὡς εἴπομεν ἀνωτέρω, παρενείρονται ἐν τῇ Ἀναστασίμῳ Ἀπολύσει, ὅταν τύχωσιν ἐν ἡμέρᾳ Κυριακῆς.

  Πλὴν τῆς μεγάλης Ἀπολύσεως, ὑπάρχει καὶ ἡ λεγομένη μικρά, ἥτις λέγεται τὸ ἑσπέρας μετὰ τὴν Θ΄ ὥραν καὶ μετὰ τὸν μικρὸν Ἑσπερινὸν ὅταν γίνηται Ἀγρυπνία, καὶ μετὰ τὸ Ἀπόδειπνον, καὶ τὸ πρωῒ μετὰ τὸ Μεσονυκτικόν. Ἔστι δὲ ἡ μικρὰ Ἀπόλυσις αὕτη· *Δόξα σοι ὁ Θεὸς ἡ ἐλπὶς ἡμῶν δόξα σοι (ὁ ἀναστὰς ἐκ νεκρῶν - ἐν Κυριακῇ) Χριστὸς ὁ ἀληθινὸς Θεὸς ἡμῶν, ταῖς πρεσβείαις τῆς παναχράντου αὐτοῦ Μητρός, τῶν ἁγίων ἐνδόξων καὶ πανευφήμων Ἀποστόλων* (τοῦ Ἁγίου τῆς ἡμέρας) *καὶ πάντων τῶν Ἁγίων ἐλεήσαι καὶ σώσαι ἡμᾶς ὡς ἀγαθὸς καὶ φιλάνθρωπος*· εἶτα τὸ *Δι᾽ εὐχῶν (τῶν ἁγίων πατέρων ἡμῶν. Κύριε Ἰησοῦ Χριστὲ ὁ Θεός, ἐλέησον ἡμᾶς. Ἀμήν.)* Ἄν ὅμως ἕπηται τὸ *Εὐξώμεθα ὑπὲρ εἰρήνης...* (ὡς μετὰ τὸ Ἀπόδειπνον καὶ μετὰ τὸ Μεσονυκτικὸν) τὸ *Δι᾽ εὐχῶν* λέγεται εἰς τὸ τέλος τοῦ *Εὐξώμεθα*.

---

## Ο ΟΡΘΡΟΣ⁸

### ΠΕΡΙ ΤΟΥ ΜΕΣΟΝΥΚΤΙΚΟΥ

**17.** Τῆς τοῦ Ὄρθρου Ἀκολουθίας προηγεῖται ἡ τοῦ Μεσονυκτικοῦ. Εἰσὶ δὲ δύο τὰ ἀναγινωσκόμενα Μεσονυκτικά, τὸ τῶν πέντε ἡμερῶν τῆς ἑβδομάδος, καὶ τὸ τοῦ Σαββάτου, ἅτινα εὕρηνται ἐν τῷ Ὡρολογίῳ. Τῇ Κυριακῇ τὸν τόπον τοῦ Μεσονυκτικοῦ ἐπέχει ἡ ἑξῆς Ἀκολουθία. Μετὰ τὸ *Εὐλογητὸς ὁ Θεὸς* ὁ Προϊστάμενος λέγει *Δόξα σοι ὁ Θεὸς ἡμῶν δόξα σοι, τὸ Βασιλεῦ Οὐράνιε Παράκλητε, τὸ Τρισάγιον, Κύριε...*

*(Printed Page 58)*

=== LEAF p74 ===

...of these Feasts occurs on a Sunday—when the Resurrection hymns take precedence—both at Saturday Vespers, at Sunday Matins, and at the Divine Liturgy, the Dismissal bears the characteristic phrases of both the Sunday and the occurring Feast, e.g.: **"Glory to Thee, O Christ our God and our hope, glory to Thee. May He Who was born in a cave and lay in a manger"** (*Ὁ ἐν σπηλαίῳ γεννηθεὶς καὶ ἐν φάτνῃ ἀνακλιθεὶς*) (or **"May He Who deigned to be baptized in the Jordan by John for our salvation"** [*Ὁ ἐν Ἰορδάνῃ ὑπὸ Ἰωάννου βαπτισθῆναι καταδεξάμενος...*]) **"and rose from the dead, Christ our true God..."** etc. On the Feast of the Universal Exaltation of the Precious Cross [September 14], the Resurrection Dismissal is said on whatever day the feast falls, seeing that at Matins we chant **"Having beheld the Resurrection of Christ"** (*Ἀνάστασιν Χριστοῦ θεασάμενοι*). It is to be noted that only two Feasts of the Mother of God—the Annunciation [March 25] and the Meeting of the Lord [February 2]—have special characteristic phrases in their Dismissal prayers, which, as stated above, are inserted into the Resurrection Dismissal when these feasts fall on a Sunday.[^74]

  In addition to the Major Dismissal, there is also the so-called Minor Dismissal, which is said in the evening after the Ninth Hour, and after Small Vespers when an All-Night Vigil is held, and after Compline, and in the morning after the Midnight Office. The Minor Dismissal is as follows: **"Glory to Thee, O Christ our God and our hope, glory to Thee. May Christ our true God (He Who rose from the dead [on Sundays]), through the intercessions of His all-pure Mother, of the holy, glorious, and all-praised Apostles (of the Saint of the day), and of all the saints, have mercy on us and save us, for He is good and the Lover of mankind."** Then **"Through the prayers..."** is recited. If, however, the litany **"Let us pray for the peace of the world..."** (*Εὐξώμεθα ὑπὲρ εἰρήνης...*) follows (as after Compline and after the Midnight Office), **"Through the prayers..."** is said at the conclusion of **"Let us pray for the peace..."**

---

## Matins[^75]

### Concerning the Midnight Office

**17.** The Midnight Office (*Mesonyktikon* / *Μεσονυκτικόν*) precedes the Service of Matins. There are two Midnight Offices that are read: that of the five weekdays, and that of Saturday, both of which are found in the Horologion. On Sunday, the place of the Midnight Office is taken by the following order of service: After **"Blessed is our God..."** (*Εὐλογητὸς ὁ Θεὸς ἡμῶν...*), the Superior [or Senior Priest] recites: **"Glory to Thee, our God, glory to Thee"** (*Δόξα σοι ὁ Θεὸς ἡμῶν, δόξα σοι*), **"O Heavenly King, Comforter"** (*Βασιλεῦ Οὐράνιε, Παράκλητε*), the Trisagion Prayers, **"Lord,...**

*(Printed Page 59)*

=== LEAF p75 ===

...ἐλέησον ιβ΄. *Δόξα πατρὶ καὶ Υἱῷ κτλ.* *Δεῦτε προσκυνήσωμεν* τρίς, καὶ τὸν Ν΄ Ψαλμόν, εἶτα ψάλλεται ὁ Τριαδικὸς κανὼν μετὰ στίχου *Ἁγία Τριὰς ὁ Θεὸς ἐλέησον καὶ σῶσον ἡμᾶς*· καὶ τὰ δ΄ Τριαδικὰ *Ἄξιόν ἐστιν*· μετὰ ταῦτα λέγεται τὸ Τρισάγιον αὖθις, καὶ ἡ Ὑπακοὴ τοῦ ἤχου· εἶτα ὁ Ἱερεὺς *Ἐλέησον ἡμᾶς ὁ Θεὸς... ἔτι δεόμεθα ὑπὲρ τοῦ Ἀρχιεπισκόπου ἡμῶν... ὑπὲρ τοῦ διαφυλαχθῆναι...* καὶ ἡ μικρὰ Ἀπόλυσις (ἴδε ταύτην ὄπισθεν ἐν §16), καὶ εὐθὺς *Εὐξώμεθα ὑπὲρ εἰρήνης τοῦ κόσμου...* κτλ. τὸ τέλος *Εἴπωμεν καὶ ὑπὲρ ἑαυτῶν τὸ Κύριε ἐλέησον, Κύριε ἐλέησον, Κύριε ἐλέησον*, καὶ εὐθὺς τὸ *Δι᾽ εὐχῶν* ⁹.

  Εἰ τύχοι ἐν τῇ Κυριακῇ Θεομητορικὴ ἑορτὴ ἢ μνήμη ἑορταζομένου Ἁγίου, μετὰ τὸν Τριαδικὸν κανόνα ψάλλεται ἡ Λιτὴ τῆς ἑορτῆς ἢ ἡ τοῦ Ἁγίου, μετ᾽ αὐτὴν τὰ *Ἄξιόν ἐστιν...* Ἐν ταῖς μεθεόρτοις Κυριακαῖς καὶ ταῖς ἐν Κυριακῇ Ἀποδόσεσι τῶν Δεσποτικῶν καὶ Θεομητορικῶν ἑορτῶν, ἀντὶ τῆς τοῦ ἤχου Ὑπακοῆς ψάλλεται τὸ Ἀπολυτίκιον τῆς ἑορτῆς· ὡσαύτως καὶ ὅταν τύχῃ ἐν Κυριακῇ ἑορταζόμενος Ἅγιος, τὸ τοῦ Ἁγίου. Ἐν ταῖς Κυριακαῖς τοῦ Τριῳδίου (ὅταν μὴ συμπίπτῃ ἐν ταύταις ἡ ἑορτὴ τῆς Ὑπαπαντῆς ἢ τοῦ Εὐαγγελισμοῦ) τῆς Γ΄ Κυριακῆς ἐξαιρουμένης, ἀντὶ τῆς Ὑπακοῆς ψάλλονται τὰ γ΄ Κατανυκτικὰ τροπάρια *Ἐλέησον ἡμᾶς Κύριε, ἐλέησον ἡμᾶς* κτλ., ἅτινα ψάλλονται καὶ ἐν τοῖς καθ᾽ ἡμέραν Ὄρθροις.

  Ἐν δὲ ταῖς ἐν Κυριακῇ συμπιπτούσαις Δεσποτικαῖς ἑορταῖς, μετὰ τὸ *Εὐλογητὸς ὁ Θεός*, τὸ *Δόξα σοι ὁ Θεός... Βασιλεῦ Οὐράνιε* κτλ. ὡς ἀνωτέρω μετὰ τὸν Ν΄ ψάλλεται ἡ Λιτὴ τῆς ἑορτῆς· εἶτα...

*(Printed Page 60)*

=== LEAF p76 ===

...have mercy"** (12 times), **"Glory to the Father, and to the Son, and to the Holy Spirit, now and forever, and unto the ages of ages. Amen."**, **"Come, let us worship"** (thrice), and Psalm 50 [Psalm 50 LXX]; then the Triadic Canon is chanted with the refrain: **"Most Holy Trinity, our God, have mercy on us and save us"** (*Ἁγία Τριὰς ὁ Θεὸς ἐλέησον καὶ σῶσον ἡμᾶς*); and then the 4 Triadic Troparia **"It is truly meet"** (*Ἄξιόν ἐστιν*); after these, the Trisagion Prayers are recited once again, and the Hypakoe (*Ὑπακοή*) of the Tone [Tone 1–8]; then the Priest intones: **"Have mercy on us, O God, according to Thy great mercy..."** (*Ἐλέησον ἡμᾶς ὁ Θεός...*), **"Again we pray for our Archbishop..."**, **"Again we pray for the protection of this holy temple..."**, and the Minor Dismissal (see above, §16); and immediately: **"Let us pray for the peace of the world..."** (*Εὐξώμεθα ὑπὲρ εἰρήνης τοῦ κόσμου...*) etc.; at the conclusion: **"Let us say also for ourselves: Lord, have mercy; Lord, have mercy; Lord, have mercy"**; and immediately **"Through the prayers of our holy fathers..."** (*Δι᾽ εὐχῶν...*).[^76]

  If on Sunday there occurs a Feast of the Mother of God or the commemoration of a celebrated Saint, after the Triadic Canon the Litiya of the feast or of the Saint is chanted, and after it **"It is truly meet..."** On Afterfeast Sundays and on Sundays upon which the Apodoses of Feasts of the Lord and of the Mother of God occur, instead of the Hypakoe of the Tone, the Dismissal Troparion of the feast is chanted; likewise, when a celebrated Saint occurs on Sunday, the Dismissal Troparion of the Saint is chanted. On the Sundays of the Triodion (when neither the Feast of the Meeting of the Lord nor the Annunciation occurs thereon), with the exception of the Third Sunday [Veneration of the Cross], instead of the Hypakoe, the 3 Penitential Troparia **"Have mercy on us, O Lord, have mercy on us"** (*Ἐλέησον ἡμᾶς, Κύριε, ἐλέησον ἡμᾶς*) etc. are chanted, which are sung also at daily Matins.

  On Feasts of the Lord occurring on a Sunday, after **"Blessed is our God..."**, **"Glory to Thee, our God... O Heavenly King..."** etc., as set forth above, after Psalm 50 the Litiya of the feast is chanted; then the Trisagion Prayers, followed by...

*(Printed Page 61)*

=== LEAF p77 ===

...λέγεται τὸ Τρισάγιον, τὸ Ἀπολυτίκιον τῆς ἑορτῆς. Ὁ Ἱερεὺς *Ἐλέησον ἡμᾶς ὁ Θεός...* ἡ Ἀπόλυσις, τὸ *Εὐξώμεθα* καὶ τὸ *Δι᾽ εὐχῶν*. Αὕτη ἡ διάταξις ἰσχύει καὶ ὅταν Δεσποτικὴ ἢ Θεομητορικὴ ἑορτή, ἢ μνήμη ἑορταζομένου Ἁγίου συμπίπτῃ ἐν ἄλλῃ ἡμέρᾳ πλὴν Κυριακῆς. Καθ᾽ ὅλην δὲ τὴν Διακαινήσιμον ἑβδομάδα Μεσονυκτικοῦ τόπον ἐπέχει τὸ *Ἀνάστασιν Χριστοῦ... Προλαβοῦσαι τὸν ὄρθρον* κτλ. ὡς καὶ κατὰ τὴν ἡμέραν τῆς Ἀποδόσεως τοῦ Πάσχα (ὅρα § 3 σημ. 4 Κυριακὴ τοῦ Πάσχα εἰς τὸ Λυχνικόν) {ἡ σωστὴ παραπομπὴ εἶναι §2, σημ. 8, Κυριακὴ τοῦ Πάσχα ἐν τῇ Λειτουργίᾳ}.

  Σημειωτέον ὅτι, τὸ *Βασιλεῦ οὐράνιε* ἐν τῇ ἀρχῇ τοῦ Ἑσπερινοῦ καὶ τοῦ Ὄρθρου οὐ λέγεται ἀπὸ τῆς Κυριακῆς τοῦ Πάσχα μέχρι τῆς Πεντηκοστῆς, οὐδὲ τὸ *Ἅγιος ὁ Θεὸς* μέχρι τῆς Ἀναλήψεως, ἀλλὰ μετὰ τὸ *Εὐλογητὸς ὁ Θεὸς* εὐθὺς *Χριστὸς ἀνέστη* τρίς, *Δόξα Πατρὶ... Καὶ νῦν καὶ ἀεὶ... Παναγία Τριὰς...* κτλ.

---

## ΠΕΡΙ ΤΟΥ ΕΞΑΨΑΛΜΟΥ

**18.** Ὁ Ἱερεὺς μετὰ τὴν Ἀπόλυσιν τοῦ Μεσονυκτικοῦ εἰσέρχεται εἰς τὸ Ἱερὸν Βῆμα, καὶ στὰς ἐνώπιον τῆς Ἁγίας Τραπέζης ἐκφωνεῖ μετ᾿ εὐλαβείας καὶ φόβου Θεοῦ, *Εὐλογητὸς ὁ Θεὸς ἡμῶν*· ὁ Ἀναγνώστης τὸ *Τρισάγιον*· ὁ Ἱερεὺς *Ὅτι σοῦ ἐστιν*· ὁ Ἀναγνώστης τὰ Τροπάρια *Σῶσον Κύριε*, *Δόξα Ὁ ὑψωθεὶς ἐν τῷ Σταυρῷ*, *Καὶ νῦν Προστασία φοβερὰ*· ὁ Ἱερεὺς *Ἐλέησον ἡμᾶς ὁ Θεὸς* κτλ. τὸ τέλος *Ὅτι ἐλεήμων καὶ φιλάνθρωπος...* καὶ ὁ Ἀναγνώστης *Ἐν ὀνόματι Κυρίου εὐλόγησον Πάτερ*· ὁ Ἱερεὺς *Δόξα τῇ Ἁγίᾳ καὶ Ὁμοουσίῳ...* κτλ., ὁ δὲ Προϊστάμενος λέγει τὸν Ἑξάψαλμον μετὰ κατανύξεως, τοῦ λαοῦ ἀκρωμένου μετὰ πάσης σιωπῆς καὶ εὐλαβείας. Ὁ Ἑξάψαλμος οὐδέποτε παραλείπεται πλὴν τῆς Διακαινησίμου ἑβδομάδος καὶ τῆς ἡμέρας τῆς Ἀποδόσεως τοῦ Πάσχα.

---

## ΠΕΡΙ ΤΟΥ «ΘΕΟΣ ΚΥΡΙΟΣ»

**19.** Τὸ *Θεὸς Κύριος* ψάλλεται τετράκις· ἀπὸ τοῦ β΄ δὲ λέγονται οἱ ὡρισμένοι στίχοι *Ἐξομολογεῖσθε τῷ Κυρίῳ καὶ ἐπικαλεῖσθε τὸ ὄνομα τὸ ἅγιον αὐτοῦ.* — *Πάντα τὰ ἔθνη...* — *Παρὰ Κυρίου ἐγένετο...* Εἶτα εὐθὺς τὸ Ἀπολυτίκιον· εἰς τὸ β΄ προηγεῖται τὸ *Δόξα πατρί...* καὶ εἰς τὸ γ΄ τὸ *Καὶ νῦν...* Τὸ ἀναστάσιμον τοῦ ἤχου Ἀπολυτίκιον εἰς τὸ *Θεὸς Κύριος* ἐν πάσαις ταῖς Κυριακαῖς ψάλλεται δίς, καὶ ἅπαξ τὸ Θεοτοκίον. Θεομητορικῆς δὲ ἑορτῆς τυχούσης, τὸ Ἀναστάσιμον...

*(Printed Page 62)*

=== LEAF p78 ===

...the Dismissal Troparion of the feast is chanted. The Priest intones: **"Have mercy on us, O God..."**, followed by the Dismissal, the litany **"Let us pray for the peace..."**, and **"Through the prayers..."** This order is likewise observed when a Feast of the Lord, a Feast of the Mother of God, or the commemoration of a celebrated Saint falls on a weekday. Throughout Bright Week, as well as on the Apodosis of Pascha, the Midnight Office is replaced by **"Having beheld the Resurrection of Christ... Anticipating the dawn..."** (*Ἀνάστασιν Χριστοῦ θεασάμενοι... Προλαβοῦσαι τὸν ὄρθρον*), etc. (see §2, Note 8, Easter Sunday at the Divine Liturgy).[^77]

  It is to be noted that **"O Heavenly King"** (*Βασιλεῦ Οὐράνιε*) is not said at the beginning of Vespers or Matins from Easter Sunday until Pentecost, nor is **"Holy God"** (*Ἅγιος ὁ Θεός*) said until the Ascension; but after **"Blessed is our God..."**, immediately **"Christ is risen from the dead"** (*Χριστὸς ἀνέστη*) is chanted thrice, followed by **"Glory to the Father, and to the Son, and to the Holy Spirit, now and forever, and unto the ages of ages. Amen."**, **"All-Holy Trinity, have mercy on us..."** (*Παναγία Τριάς...*), etc.

---

## Concerning the Six Psalms (*Hexapsalmos*)[^78]

**18.** The Priest, after the Dismissal of the Midnight Office, enters the Sanctuary, and standing before the Holy Table proclaims with reverence and fear of God: **"Blessed is our God, always, now and forever, and unto the ages of ages"** (*Εὐλογητὸς ὁ Θεὸς ἡμῶν...*); the Reader recites the Trisagion Prayers; the Priest: **"For Thine is the kingdom..."** (*Ὅτι σοῦ ἐστιν ἡ βασιλεία...*); the Reader chants the Troparia: **"O Lord, save Thy people"** (*Σῶσον, Κύριε, τὸν λαόν σου*), **"Glory..."**, **"Thou Who wast lifted up upon the Cross"** (*Ὁ ὑψωθεὶς ἐν τῷ Σταυρῷ*), **"Now and forever..."**, **"O fearsome Protection"** (*Προστασία φοβερὰ*); the Priest intones: **"Have mercy on us, O God..."** etc., concluding with the ecphonesis: **"For Thou art a merciful God, and the Lover of mankind..."** (*Ὅτι ἐλεήμων καὶ φιλάνθρωπος Θεὸς ὑπάρχεις...*); and the Reader says: **"In the Name of the Lord, bless, Father"** (*Ἐν ὀνόματι Κυρίου εὐλόγησον, Πάτερ*); the Priest: **"Glory to the Holy, Consubstantial, Life-Creating, and Undivided Trinity, always, now and forever, and unto the ages of ages"** (*Δόξα τῇ Ἁγίᾳ καὶ Ὁμοουσίῳ καὶ Ζωοποιῷ καὶ Ἀδιαιρέτῳ Τριάδι...*); and the Superior [or Senior Priest] reads the Six Psalms (*Ἑξάψαλμος*) with compunction, while the people listen in complete silence and reverence. The Six Psalms are never omitted except during Bright Week and on the Apodosis of Pascha.

---

## Concerning "God is the Lord"[^79]

**19.** **"God is the Lord, and hath appeared unto us; blessed is He that cometh in the Name of the Lord"** (*Θεὸς Κύριος καὶ ἐπέφανεν ἡμῖν*, Psalm 117:27, 26 LXX) is chanted four times; from the second time onward, the appointed verses are intoned:
1. **"Give thanks unto the Lord, and call upon His holy Name"** (*Ἐξομολογεῖσθε τῷ Κυρίῳ, καὶ ἐπικαλεῖσθε τὸ ὄνομα τὸ ἅγιον αὐτοῦ*, Psalm 117:1 LXX).
2. **"All nations compassed me about, but in the Name of the Lord I drove them back"** (*Πάντα τὰ ἔθνη ἐκύκλωσάν με...*, Psalm 117:10 LXX).
3. **"This is the Lord's doing, and it is marvelous in our eyes"** (*Παρὰ Κυρίου ἐγένετο αὕτη, καὶ ἔστι θαυμαστὴ ἐν ὀφθαλμοῖς ἡμῶν*, Psalm 117:23 LXX).

Then immediately the Dismissal Troparion is chanted; at the second singing, it is preceded by **"Glory to the Father, and to the Son, and to the Holy Spirit"**, and at the third by **"Now and forever, and unto the ages of ages. Amen."** On all Sundays, at **"God is the Lord,"** the Resurrection Dismissal Troparion of the Tone is chanted twice, and the Theotokion once. If a Feast of the Mother of God occurs, the Resurrection Troparion is chanted once,...

*(Printed Page 63)*

=== LEAF p79 ===

...ἅπαξ καὶ τὸ τῆς ἑορτῆς δίς· ἐπίσης καὶ ἄν συμπέσῃ ἐν Κυριακῇ Ἀπόδοσις Δεσποτικῆς ἢ Θεομητορικῆς ἑορτῆς. Ἐν ταῖς μεθεόρτοις Κυριακαῖς τὸ Ἀναστάσιμον δίς, καὶ τὸ τῆς ἑορτῆς ἅπαξ. Εἰ τύχοι δὲ καὶ ἑορταζόμενος Ἅγιος, λέγεται ἅπαξ καὶ τὸ ἀναστάσιμον Ἀπολυτίκιον, καὶ τὸ τοῦ Ἁγίου, καὶ τὸ τῆς ἑορτῆς. Ἐὰν δὲ ᾖ μόνος ὁ Ἅγιος ἐν τῇ Κυριακῇ, ψάλλονται ἀνὰ μίαν ὡσαύτως φορὰν τὸ Ἀναστάσιμον, τὸ τοῦ Ἁγίου καὶ Θεοτοκίον σύμφωνον τῷ ἤχῳ τοῦ Ἀπολυτικίου τοῦ Ἁγίου. Ἐν ταῖς νηστησίμοις τῆς Μ. Τεσσαρακοστῆς ἡμέραις *Θεὸς Κύριος* οὐ ψάλλεται, ἀλλ᾿ ἀντὶ αὐτοῦ ψάλλεται τὸ *Ἀλληλούϊα* τετράκις ἀνὰ τρὶς εἰς ἦχον πλ. δ΄ μετὰ τῶν ἑξῆς στίχων·

  α΄. *Ἐκ νυκτὸς ὀρθρίζει τὸ πνεῦμά μου πρὸς σὲ ὁ Θεός, διότι φῶς τὰ προστάγματά σου ἐπὶ τῆς γῆς.*  
  *Ἀλληλούϊα τρίς*

  β΄. *Δικαιοσύνην μάθετε οἱ ἐνοικοῦντες ἐπὶ τῆς γῆς.*  
  *Ἀλληλούϊα τρίς*

  γ΄. *Ζῆλος λήψεται λαὸν ἀπαίδευτον, καὶ νῦν πῦρ τοὺς ὑπεναντίους ἔδεται.*  
  *Ἀλληλούϊα τρίς*

  δ΄. *Πρόσθες αὐτοῖς κακά, Κύριε, πρόσθες αὐτοῖς κακὰ τοῖς ἐνδόξοις τῆς γῆς.*  
  *Ἀλληλούϊα τρίς*

  Σημειωτέον ὅτι τὰ Σάββατα, καθ᾽ ἅ οὐ τυγχάνει ἑορτὴ ἐπισήμου Ἁγίου, ἀντὶ τοῦ *Θεὸς Κύριος* ψάλλεται τὸ *Ἀλληλούϊα* μετὰ τῶν ὡρισμένων στίχων αὐτοῦ (*Μακάροι οὓς ἐξελέξω... Καὶ τὸ μνημόσυνον αὐτῶν*) καὶ λέγονται τὰ γνωστὰ Ἀπολυτίκια *Ἀπόστολοι μάρτυρες... Δόξα..., Μνήσθητι Κύριε· Καὶ νῦν..., Μήτηρ ἁγία*.

  Τὰ δύο Σάββατα τῶν ψυχῶν ἐν μὲν τῷ ἑσπερινῷ τὸ *Ὁ βάθυ σοφίας, Δόξα* τὸ τέλος. *Καὶ νῦν Σὲ καὶ τεῖχος καὶ λιμένα*, ἐν δὲ τῷ Ὄρθρῳ τὰ αὐτά, προψαλλομένου τοῦ *Ἀλληλούϊα* μετὰ τῶν Στίχων.

*(Printed Page 64)*

=== LEAF p80 ===

...and that of the feast twice; likewise if the Apodosis of a Feast of the Lord or of the Mother of God falls on a Sunday. On Afterfeast Sundays, the Resurrection Troparion is chanted twice, and that of the feast once. If a celebrated Saint also occurs, the Resurrection Dismissal Troparion, that of the Saint, and that of the feast are each chanted once. If only the Saint occurs on Sunday, the Resurrection Troparion, that of the Saint, and the Theotokion in the same Tone as the Dismissal Troparion of the Saint are chanted once each. On the fasting days of Great Lent [Monday through Friday], **"God is the Lord"** is not chanted; instead, **"Alleluia"** (*Ἀλληλούϊα*) is chanted four times (thrice after each verse) in Tone 8 [Tone 4 Plagal] with the following verses:

  1. **"From the night my spirit waketh early unto Thee, O God, for Thy commandments are a light upon the earth"** (*Ἐκ νυκτὸς ὀρθρίζει τὸ πνεῦμά μου πρὸς σὲ ὁ Θεός, διότι φῶς τὰ προστάγματά σου ἐπὶ τῆς γῆς*, Isaiah 26:9 LXX).  
     **"Alleluia"** [thrice]

  2. **"Learn righteousness, ye that dwell upon the earth"** (*Δικαιοσύνην μάθετε οἱ ἐνοικοῦντες ἐπὶ τῆς γῆς*, Isaiah 26:9 LXX).  
     **"Alleluia"** [thrice]

  3. **"Jealousy shall seize upon an untaught people, and now fire shall devour the adversaries"** (*Ζῆλος λήψεται λαὸν ἀπαίδευτον, καὶ νῦν πῦρ τοὺς ὑπεναντίους ἔδεται*, Isaiah 26:11 LXX).  
     **"Alleluia"** [thrice]

  4. **"Add evils unto them, O Lord, add evils unto them, unto the glorious of the earth"** (*Πρόσθες αὐτοῖς κακά, Κύριε, πρόσθες αὐτοῖς κακὰ τοῖς ἐνδόξοις τῆς γῆς*, Isaiah 26:15 LXX).  
     **"Alleluia"** [thrice][^80]

  It is to be noted that on Saturdays upon which there is no commemoration of an illustrious Saint, instead of **"God is the Lord,"** **"Alleluia"** is chanted with its appointed verses (**"Blessed are they whom Thou hast chosen and taken to Thyself, O Lord"** [*Μακάριοι οὓς ἐξελέξω...*, Psalm 64:5 LXX] and **"And their memorial is from generation to generation"** [*Καὶ τὸ μνημόσυνον αὐτῶν...*, Psalm 101:13 LXX]), and the customary Dismissal Troparia are chanted: **"Apostles, Martyrs, and Prophets"** (*Ἀπόστολοι, μάρτυρες...*), **"Glory..."**, **"Remember, O Lord, as Thou art good, Thy servants"** (*Μνήσθητι, Κύριε, ὡς ἀγαθὸς...*), **"Now and forever..."**, **"Holy Mother of the ineffable Light"** (*Μήτηρ ἁγία ἡ τοῦ ἀφράστου φωτός*).

  On the two Saturdays of Souls [Meatfare Saturday and the Saturday before Pentecost], at Vespers: **"O Thou Who with profound wisdom"** (*Ὁ βάθει σοφίας*), **"Glory..."**, repeat the conclusion of the troparion **"O Thou Who with profound wisdom,"** **"Now and forever..."**, **"In Thee we have a wall and a haven"** (*Σὲ καὶ τεῖχος καὶ λιμένα*); at Matins these same troparia are chanted, preceded by **"Alleluia"** with its verses as shown above.[^81]

## ΠΕΡΙ ΤΟΥ ΨΑΛΤΗΡΙΟΥ

**20.** Τὸ Ψαλτήριον κατὰ τὴν ἀρχαίαν τυπικὴν διάταξιν στιχολογεῖται πρὸ τῶν Καθισμάτων ἑκάστης Στιχολογίας, ὁ δὲ Πολυέλεος (ὅταν ὑπάρχῃ) πρὸ τοῦ Καθίσματος τῆς γ΄ Στιχολογίας, τῆς τυχούσης ἑορτῆς· ἀλλὰ νῦν ἐνιαχοῦ πρὸς οἰκονομίαν καιροῦ κατὰ τὰς Κυριακὰς καὶ ἑορτασίμους ἡμέρας ἀναγινώσκονται πρὸ τοῦ Ὄρθρου τὰ ὡρισμένα τῆς ἡμέρας Καθίσματα τοῦ Ψαλτηρίου ὡς καὶ ὁ Πολυέλεος (ὅταν εἶνε ἑορτὴ ἔχουσα Λιτήν) ἢ ὁ Ἄμωμος (ὅταν εἶνε ψιλὴ Κυριακὴ μετὰ Ἁγίου μὴ ἑορταζομένου). Ἔστι δὲ Πολυέλεος τῶν μὲν Δεσποτικῶν ἑορτῶν οἱ Ψαλμοὶ ΡΛΔ΄ (134) καὶ ΡΛΕ΄ (135) *Αἰνεῖτε τὸ ὄνομα Κυρίου*, καὶ τὸ *Ἐξομολογεῖσθε τῷ Κυρίῳ*, τῶν δὲ Θεομητορικῶν ἑορτῶν, ὁ ψαλμὸς ΜΔ΄ (44) *Ἐξηρεύξατο ἡ καρδία μου*, τῶν δὲ ἑορταζομένων Ἁγίων οἱ ἔχοντες Ἐκλογάριον εὑρίσκουσιν ἐν αὐτῷ καταλλήλους Πολυελέους δι᾽ ἑκάστην ἑορτήν, εἰδεμή, λεγέτωσαν τοὺς ψαλμ. ΡΛΔ΄ (134) καὶ ΡΛΕ΄ (135). Ὁ ψαλμὸς ΡΛΣΤ΄ (136) *Ἐπὶ τὸν ποταμὸν Βαβυλῶνος* ἀναγινώσκεται τῇ Κυριακῇ τῆς Ἀπόκρεω καὶ τῆς Τυρινῆς. Στιχολογοῦνται δὲ καθ᾽ ἑκάστην ἡμέραν ἀνὰ δύο Καθίσματα τοῦ Ψαλτηρίου, καὶ τῇ Μ. Τεσσαρακοστῇ ἀνὰ τρία· ἀλλὰ τὴν μὲν Α΄ ἑβδομάδα τῆς Τεσσαρακοστῆς πληροῦται δὶς ὁλόκληρον τὸ Ψαλτήριον, ἀπὸ δὲ τοῦ ἑσπερινοῦ τῶν Βαΐων μέχρι τῆς Μ. Τετάρτης πληροῦται ἅπαξ. Οὐ στιχολογεῖται δὲ ἀπὸ τῆς Μ. Πέμπτης μέχρι τοῦ Σαββάτου τῆς Διακαινησίμου ἑβδομάδος.

## ΠΕΡΙ ΤΩΝ ΚΑΘΙΣΜΑΤΩΝ

**21.** Θεομητορικῆς ἑορτῆς ἐν ἡμέρᾳ Κυριακῇ συμπιπτούσης, ψάλλονται Καθίσματα εἰς τὴν α΄ καὶ β΄ Στιχολογίαν ἀνὰ δύο Ἀναστάσιμα καὶ ἀνὰ ἓν τῆς ἑορτῆς ἀντὶ τῶν Θεοτοκίων τῆς Ὀκτωήχου. Εἰς δὲ τὴν γ΄ Στιχολογίαν μόνα τὰ τῆς ἑορτῆς. Ἐν ταῖς μεθεόρτοις Κυριακαῖς καὶ ταῖς Ἀποδόσεσι ψάλλονται ἐν τῇ α΄ καὶ β΄ Στιχολογίᾳ πάλιν ἀνὰ δύο Ἀναστάσιμα καὶ ἀντὶ Θεοτοκίων λέγονται τὰ τῆς ἑορτῆς. Γ΄ Στιχολογίας Κάθισμα οὐχ ὑπάρχει τότε. Ἐν ταῖς μνήμαις τῶν ἧττον ἐπισήμως ἑορταζομένων Ἁγίων μετὰ τὰ Ἀναστάσιμα καὶ τὰ Θεοτοκία τῆς α΄ καὶ β΄ Στιχολογίας, ψάλλονται τὰ Καθίσματα τῆς γ΄ Στιχολογίας τοῦ Ἁγίου· ἐν δὲ ταῖς μνήμαις τῶν ἐπισημότερον ἑορταζομένων Ἁγίων καὶ ἐχόντων Λιτὴν καὶ Πολυελέου Καθίσματα, οἷον τῶν Ἀποστόλων, τοῦ Προδρόμου, τῶν...

## Concerning the Psalter

**20.** According to the ancient typikon order, the Psalter is chanted in verses (*stichologeitai*) before the Kathismata [Sessional Hymns] of each Stichologia; the Polyeleos (whenever appointed) is chanted before the Kathisma of the third Stichologia of the occurring feast. Now, however, in certain places, by pastoral economy of time, on Sundays and festal days the appointed Kathismata of the Psalter for the day, as well as the Polyeleos (when there is a feast having a Litiya) or the Amomos [Psalm 118 LXX] (when it is an ordinary Sunday with a non-celebrated Saint), are read before Matins. The Polyeleos of Feasts of the Lord consists of Psalm 134 [MT 135] **"Praise the Name of the Lord"** (*Αἰνεῖτε τὸ ὄνομα Κυρίου*) and Psalm 135 [MT 136] **"Give thanks unto the Lord, for He is good"** (*Ἐξομολογεῖσθε τῷ Κυρίῳ*); for Feasts of the Mother of God, the Polyeleos consists solely of Psalm 44 [MT 45] **"My heart hath poured forth a good word"** (*Ἐξηρεύξατο ἡ καρδία μου*); for celebrated Saints, those who possess an *Eklogarion* find in it the appropriate Polyeleos selections for each feast; otherwise, let them recite Psalms 134 [MT 135] and 135 [MT 136]. Psalm 136 [MT 137] **"By the rivers of Babylon"** (*Ἐπὶ τῶν ποταμῶν Βαβυλῶνος*) is read on Meatfare Sunday and Cheesefare Sunday. On each day, two Kathismata of the Psalter are chanted in verses, and during Great Lent three are chanted; but during the First Week of Great Lent the entire Psalter is completed twice, and from Vespers of Palm Sunday until Holy Wednesday it is completed once. The Psalter is not chanted in verses from Holy Thursday until Saturday of Bright Week.[^82]

---

## Concerning the Kathismata [Sessional Hymns]

**21.** When a Feast of the Mother of God coincides with a Sunday, Kathismata are chanted at the first and second Stichologiae: two Resurrection Kathismata and one of the feast instead of the Theotokia of the Octoechos. At the third Stichologia, only the Kathismata of the feast are chanted. On Afterfeast Sundays and on Apodoses, two Resurrection Kathismata are chanted again at the first and second Stichologiae, and those of the feast are said instead of the Theotokia. There is no Kathisma of the third Stichologia on those occasions. On commemorations of less solemnly celebrated Saints, after the Resurrection Kathismata and Theotokia of the first and second Stichologiae, the Kathismata of the third Stichologia of the Saint are chanted; but on commemorations of more solemnly celebrated Saints having a Litiya and Polyeleos Kathismata—such as the Apostles, the Forerunner, the Three...

*(Printed Page 67)*

=== LEAF p83 ===

...τριῶν Ἱεραρχῶν, καὶ τῶν Μεγαλομαρτύρων, ψάλλεται καὶ εἰς τὰς δύο πρώτας Στιχολογίας μετὰ τὸ Ἀναστάσιμον ἀνὰ ἓν Κάθισμα τοῦ Ἁγίου, καὶ ἕπεται τὸ Θεοτοκίον· ψάλλεται δὲ ὁλόκληρος ἐν τέλει ἡ γ΄ Στιχολογία τοῦ Ἁγίου διὰ τὸν Πολυέλεον.

## ΠΕΡΙ ΤΩΝ ΕΥΛΟΓΗΤΑΡΙΩΝ
### ΤΗΣ ΥΠΑΚΟΗΣ, ΤΩΝ ΑΝΑΒΑΘΜΩΝ ΚΑΙ ΤΟΥ ΠΡΟΚΕΙΜΕΝΟΥ

**22.** Κατὰ πᾶσαν Κυριακήν, μετὰ τὰ Καθίσματα, ψάλλονται τὰ Εὐλογητάρια, εἶτα γίνεται παρὰ τοῦ Ἱερέως Αἴτησις καὶ ἡ Ἐκφώνησις *Ὅτι ηὐλόγηται*, μετὰ ταῦτα ἡ Ὑπακοὴ τοῦ ἤχου, τὰ Ἀντίφωνα, τὸ Προκείμενον καὶ ἀκολούθως οἱ Κανόνες. Θεομητορικῆς δὲ ἑορτῆς συμπιπτούσης ἐν Κυριακῇ, Εὐλογητάρια οὐ ψάλλονται· ἀλλὰ μετὰ τὰ Καθίσματα, εὐθὺς ἡ παρὰ τοῦ Ἱερέως Αἴτησις καὶ ἡ Ἐκφώνησις· εἶτα ἡ Ὑπακοὴ τοῦ ἤχου καὶ οἱ Ἀναβαθμοί, τὸ Προκείμενον τῆς ἑορτῆς, τὸ *Πᾶσα πνοὴ* καὶ τὸ Εὐαγγέλιον τοῦ Ὄρθρου τῆς ἑορτῆς· ἔπειτα τὸ *Ἀνάστασιν Χριστοῦ* καὶ ὁ Ν΄ Ψαλμὸς χῦμα, *Δόξα, Ταῖς τῆς Θεοτόκου, Καὶ νῦν* τὸ αὐτό, ὁ Στίχος *Ἐλέησόν με ὁ Θεός*, καὶ τὸ ὡρισμένον Ἰδιόμελον τῆς ἑορτῆς, μεθ᾽ ὅ ὁ Ἱερεὺς *Σῶσον ὁ Θεὸς τὸν λαόν σου... Ἐλέει καὶ οἰκτιρμοῖς*, καὶ ἄρχονται οἱ Κανόνες. Εἰς τὰς Ἀποδόσεις ὅμως καὶ τὰς μεθεόρτους Κυριακὰς τῶν Θεομητορικῶν ἢ τῶν Δεσποτικῶν ἑορτῶν, διατηρεῖται ἡ καθωρισμένη τάξις τοῦ Ἑωθινοῦ Εὐαγγελίου τῆς Κυριακῆς· (Ἴδε τὴν ἰδιαιτέραν τῆς Κυριακῆς Τυπικὴν Διάταξιν).

## ΠΕΡΙ ΤΩΝ ΚΑΝΟΝΩΝ

**23.** Οἱ τῆς Κυριακῆς ἀναστάσιμοι τῆς Ὀκτωήχου Κανόνες οὐδέποτε καταλιμπάνονται, κἂν τύχωσι Θεομητορικαὶ ἑορταὶ καὶ Ἀποδόσεις Δεσποτικῶν ἢ Θεομητορικῶν ἑορτῶν ἐν Κυριακῇ· οὐ ψάλλονται δὲ οἱ ἀναστάσιμοι τῆς Ὀκτωήχου Κανόνες ἐν ταῖς ἐφεξῆς Κυριακαῖς, ἤτοι τῇ Γ΄ Κυριακῇ τῶν Νηστειῶν καὶ τῇ πρὸ τῆς Χριστοῦ Γεννήσεως, καθ᾽ ἃς ψάλλονται οἱ Κανόνες τῆς ἡμέρας, ὧν τὰ μετὰ τὸν Εἱρμὸν πρῶτα Τροπάριά εἰσιν Ἀναστάσιμα, διὸ καὶ λέγομεν στίχον *Δόξα τῇ ἁγίᾳ Ἀναστάσει σου Κύριε*, εἰς τὰ λοιπὰ δὲ Τροπάρια τῆς μὲν Γ΄ τῶν Νηστειῶν Κυριακῆς *Δόξα σοι ὁ Θεὸς ἡμῶν δόξα σοι* τῆς...

...Hierarchs, and the Great Martyrs—at the first two Stichologiae, after the Resurrection Kathisma, one Kathisma of the Saint is chanted, followed by the Theotokion; and at the end the entire third Stichologia of the Saint is chanted for the Polyeleos.[^83]

## Concerning the Evlogitaria, the Hypakoe, the Gradual Antiphons, and the Prokimenon

**22.** Every Sunday, after the Kathismata [Sessional Hymns], the Resurrectional Evlogitaria (**"Blessed art Thou, O Lord, teach me Thy statutes"** [*Εὐλογητὸς εἶ, Κύριε*]) are chanted; then the Little Litany is intoned by the Priest with the exclamation **"For blessed is Thy Name, and glorified is Thy kingdom..."** (*Ὅτι ηὐλόγηται τὸ ὄνομά σου...*); thereafter follow the Hypakoe of the Tone, the Gradual Antiphons (*Anavathmoi*), the Prokimenon, and subsequently the Canons. When a Feast of the Mother of God coincides with a Sunday, the Evlogitaria are not chanted; instead, immediately following the Kathismata, the Little Litany and exclamation are made by the Priest; then follow the Hypakoe of the Tone, the Gradual Antiphons, the Prokimenon of the feast, **"Let every breath praise the Lord"** (*Πᾶσα πνοὴ αἰνεσάτω τὸν Κύριον*), and the Matins Gospel of the feast; thereafter **"Having beheld the Resurrection of Christ"** (*Ἀνάστασιν Χριστοῦ θεασάμενοι*) and Psalm 50 LXX read smoothly without singing (*chuma* / *χῦμα*), **"Glory..."**, **"Through the intercessions of the Theotokos, O Merciful One, blot out the multitude of our transgressions"** (*Ταῖς τῆς Θεοτόκου πρεσβείαις...*), **"Now and forever..."**, the same; the verse **"Have mercy on me, O God, according to Thy great mercy..."** (Psalm 50:1 LXX), and the appointed Idiomelon of the feast; whereafter the Priest intones **"O God, save Thy people, and bless Thine inheritance..."** (*Σῶσον, ὁ Θεός, τὸν λαόν σου...*), exclamation: **"Through the mercies and compassions and love for mankind of Thine Only-Begotten Son..."** (*Ἐλέει καὶ οἰκτιρμοῖς...*), and the Canons begin. On Apodoses and Afterfeast Sundays of Feasts of the Mother of God or Feasts of the Lord, however, the established order of the Sunday Eothinon [Morning] Gospel is preserved (see the particular Typikon Rubrics for Sunday).[^84]

---

## Concerning the Canons

**23.** The Sunday Resurrection Canons of the Octoechos are never omitted, even if Feasts of the Mother of God and Apodoses of Feasts of the Lord or Feasts of the Mother of God fall on a Sunday. The Resurrection Canons of the Octoechos are not chanted, however, on the following Sundays: namely, the Third Sunday of Lent [Veneration of the Precious Cross] and the Sunday before the Nativity of Christ [Sunday of the Holy Fathers], on which the Canons of the day are chanted, whose first troparia following the Heirmos are Resurrectional, and for this reason we intone the verse **"Glory to Thy holy Resurrection, O Lord"** (*Δόξα τῇ ἁγίᾳ Ἀναστάσει σου, Κύριε*); for the remaining troparia on the Third Sunday of Lent we chant **"Glory to Thee, our God, glory to Thee"** (*Δόξα σοι ὁ Θεὸς ἡμῶν, δόξα σοι*); and on the Sunday before the Nativity of Christ we chant **"Saints...

*(Printed Page 69)*

=== LEAF p85 ===

...δὲ πρὸ τῆς Χριστοῦ Γεννήσεως *Ἅγιοι τοῦ Θεοῦ πρεσβεύσατε ὑπὲρ ἡμῶν*. Οὐ ψάλλονται ὡσαύτως οἱ τῆς Ὀκτωήχου ἀναστάσιμοι Κανόνες καὶ εἰς τὰς μετὰ τὴν τοῦ Θωμᾶ Κυριακὴν τέσσαρας Κυριακὰς ἤτοι τῶν Μυροφόρων, τοῦ Παραλύτου, τῆς Σαμαρείτιδος καὶ τοῦ Τυφλοῦ, καθότι ψάλλεται ὁ ἀναστάσιμος τοῦ Πάσχα Κανὼν μετὰ τῶν δύο αὐτοῦ Θεοτοκίων. Ὅταν δὲ συμπέσῃ ἐν Κυριακῇ Θεομητορικὴ ἑορτὴ ἢ καὶ Ἀπόδοσις αὐτῆς, ὡς καὶ Ἀπόδοσις Δεσποτικῆς ἑορτῆς, πρῶτον ψάλλεται ὁ τῆς Ὀκτωήχου Κανὼν μετὰ τοῦ Εἱρμοῦ αὐτοῦ εἰς δ΄, εἶτα ὁ τῆς ἑορτῆς· ἂν ὅμως ἔχῃ ἡ ἑορτὴ δύο Κανόνας, ψάλλονται ἀμφότεροι μετὰ τοῦ Ἀναστασίμου εἰς ιβ΄. ἐν δὲ μεθεόρτῳ Κυριακῇ ὁ Ἀναστάσιμος καὶ ὁ εἷς ἐκ τῶν δύο τῆς ἑορτῆς. Ὅταν τύχῃ ἐν Κυριακῇ ἑορταζομένου Ἁγίου μνήμη, ψάλλονται ἐκ τοῦ ἀναστασίμου Κανόνος μετὰ τοῦ Εἱρμοῦ ἑκάστης Ὠδῆς δ΄ καὶ τοῦ Ἁγίου δ΄. Ὅταν ἡ μνήμη Ἁγίου συμπίπτῃ ἐν πάσῃ ἄλλῃ ἡμέρᾳ πλὴν Κυριακῆς, εἰ μέν εἰσι μεθέορτα, ψάλλονται τῆς ἑορτῆς μετὰ τοῦ Εἱρμοῦ δ΄ καὶ δ΄ τοῦ Ἁγίου, εἰ δὲ μή, ψάλλεται ὁ μικρὸς Παρακλητικὸς κανὼν μετὰ τοῦ Εἱρμοῦ εἰς δ΄ καὶ ὁ τοῦ Ἁγίου εἰς δ΄· ἐξαιροῦνται αἱ ἑορταὶ τῶν ἑξῆς Ἁγίων, τῶν ἀποστόλων Πέτρου καὶ Παύλου, τοῦ Εὐαγγελιστοῦ Ἰωάννου (Σεπτ. κς΄), τοῦ Γενεσίου τοῦ Προδρόμου, τῶν τριῶν Ἱεραρχῶν, τοῦ ἀποστόλου Ἀνδρέου καὶ τῶν ἁγίων Δημητρίου καὶ Νικολάου, ὧν εἰς τοὺς κανόνας προηγοῦνται Κανόνες τῆς Θεοτόκου ἰδιαίτεροι. Ἐξαιροῦνται ὡσαύτως καὶ αἱ τῶν ἁγίων Γεωργίου, Μάρκου τοῦ ἀποστόλου, Ἀθανασίου τοῦ Μεγάλου, Ἰωάννου τοῦ Θεολόγου (Μαΐου η΄) καὶ τῶν ἁγίων Κωνσταντίνου καὶ Ἑλένης, εἰς ὧν τοὺς κανόνας προτάττονται οἱ τῶν προηγηθεισῶν ἑορτῶν ἢ τῶν Κυριακῶν τοῦ Πεντηκοσταρίου.

---

## ΠΕΡΙ ΤΩΝ ΣΤΙΧΩΝ ΤΩΝ ΨΑΛΛΟΜΕΝΩΝ
### ΠΡΟ ΤΩΝ ΤΡΟΠΑΡΙΩΝ ΤΩΝ ΚΑΝΟΝΩΝ

**24.** Κατὰ τὴν ἀρχαίαν τυπικὴν διάταξιν, εἰς τοὺς ἐν τῷ Ὄρθρῳ Κανόνας τῶν Κυριακῶν καὶ τῶν Ἁγίων, καὶ εἰς τοὺς καθ᾽ ἑκάστην ἀναγινωσκομένους Κανόνας, προεψάλλοντο Στίχοι ἐκ τῶν ἐννέα Ὠδῶν τοσοῦτοι, ὅσα καὶ Τροπάρια ἐμπεριείχοντο ἐν ἑκάστῃ Ὠδῇ τοῦ Κανόνος· ἀλλὰ νῦν, μετὰ τὸν Εἱρμὸν ἑκάστης Ὠδῆς, προψάλλονται τῶν Τροπαρίων Στίχοι, εἰς μὲν τὰ Ἀναστάσιμα, *Δόξα τῇ Ἁγίᾳ Ἀναστάσει Σου Κύριε*, εἰς τὰ Θεομητορικά, *Ὑπεραγία Θεοτόκε σῶσον ἡμᾶς*, καὶ εἰς τὰ τῶν Ἁγίων, *Ἅγιοι τοῦ Θεοῦ πρεσβεύσατε ὑπὲρ ἡμῶν*. Εἰς τὸν Μέγαν κανόνα, *Ἐλέησόν με ὁ Θεὸς ἐλέησόν με*...

*(Printed Page 70)*

=== LEAF p86 ===

...of God, intercede for us"** (*Ἅγιοι τοῦ Θεοῦ, πρεσβεύσατε ὑπὲρ ἡμῶν*). Likewise, the Resurrection Canons of the Octoechos are not chanted on the four Sundays following Thomas Sunday—namely, of the Myrrhbearers, of the Paralytic, of the Samaritan Woman, and of the Blind Man—inasmuch as the Paschal Resurrection Canon is chanted with its two Theotokia. When a Feast of the Mother of God or its Apodosis, as well as the Apodosis of a Feast of the Lord, coincides with a Sunday, the Canon of the Octoechos is chanted first with its Heirmos to four verses, and then that of the feast; if, however, the feast has two Canons, both are chanted with that of the Resurrection to twelve verses; but on an Afterfeast Sunday, the Resurrection Canon and one of the two Canons of the feast are chanted. When the commemoration of a celebrated Saint falls on a Sunday, four troparia are chanted from the Resurrection Canon with the Heirmos of each Ode, and four of the Saint. When the commemoration of a Saint coincides with any other day except Sunday: if it is an Afterfeast period, four troparia of the feast are chanted with the Heirmos, and four of the Saint; if not, the Small Paraklesis Canon is chanted with the Heirmos to four verses, and that of the Saint to four. The feasts of the following Saints are excepted: the Apostles Peter and Paul [June 29], John the Evangelist (September 26), the Nativity of the Forerunner [June 24], the Three Hierarchs [January 30], the Apostle Andrew [November 30], and Saints Demetrius [October 26] and Nicholas [December 6], in whose Canons proper Canons of the Theotokos take precedence. Likewise excepted are the feasts of Saint George [April 23], Mark the Apostle [April 25], Athanasius the Great [May 2 / January 18], John the Theologian (May 8), and Saints Constantine and Helen [May 21], before whose Canons are prefixed those of the preceding feasts or of the Sundays of the Pentecostarion.[^85]

---

## Concerning the Verses Sung Before the Troparia of the Canons

**24.** According to the ancient typikon order, at Matins for the Canons of Sundays and of the Saints, and for the Canons read daily, as many verses from the Nine Biblical Canticles (*αἱ ἐννέα Ὠδαί*) were chanted beforehand as there were troparia contained in each Ode of the Canon. Now, however, after the Heirmos of each Ode, verses are chanted before the troparia: for the Resurrection troparia, **"Glory to Thy holy Resurrection, O Lord"** (*Δόξα τῇ ἁγίᾳ Ἀναστάσει σου, Κύριε*); for Marian troparia, **"Most Holy Theotokos, save us"** (*Ὑπεραγία Θεοτόκε, σῶσον ἡμᾶς*); and for the troparia of Saints, **"Saints of God, intercede for us"** (*Ἅγιοι τοῦ Θεοῦ, πρεσβεύσατε ὑπὲρ ἡμῶν*). At the Great Canon [of St. Andrew of Crete]: **"Have mercy on me, O God, have mercy on me"** (*Ἐλέησόν με, ὁ Θεός, ἐλέησόν με*); before the Martyrika preceding the Funeral Troparia: **"Through the intercessions of Thy Martyrs, O Christ God, grant rest to the souls...

*(Printed Page 71)*

=== LEAF p87 ===

...εἰς τὰ πρὸ τῶν Νεκρωσίμων Μαρτυρικά, *Πρεσβείαις τῶν Μαρτύρων σου, Χριστὲ ὁ Θεός, ἀνάπαυσον τὰς ψυχὰς τῶν δούλων*· εἰς τὰ Νεκρώσιμα, *Μακάριοι οὓς ἐξελέξω καὶ προσελάβου Κύριε*, ἢ *Αἱ ψυχαὶ αὐτῶν ἐν ἀγαθοῖς αὐλισθήσονται*, ἢ *Καὶ τὸ Μνημόσυνον αὐτῶν εἰς γενεὰν καὶ γενεάν*. Εἰς τὰ Κατανυκτικὰ τροπάρια τῆς Παρακλητικῆς, τοῦ Τριῳδίου, ὡς καὶ τῶν προεορτίων καὶ μεθεορτίων τῶν Δεσποτικῶν ἑορτῶν, τὸ *Δόξα σοι ὁ Θεὸς ἡμῶν δόξα σοι*, καὶ εἰς τὰ Μαρτυρικὰ τῆς Παρακλητικῆς τὸ *Ἅγιοι τοῦ Θεοῦ πρεσβεύσατε ὑπὲρ ἡμῶν*. Σημειωτέον δὲ ὅτι, ἐπειδὴ ἐν ταῖς μεγάλαις Δεσποτικαῖς ἑορταῖς οὐ στιχολογοῦνται κατὰ τὴν ἀρχαίαν τυπικὴν διάταξιν αἱ θ΄ Ὠδαί, διὰ τοῦτο οὐδεὶς Στίχος προψάλλεται τῶν Τροπαρίων πασῶν τῶν Δεσποτικῶν ἑορτῶν (πλὴν τῆς τοῦ Πάσχα, ὅτε λέγεται τὸ *Δόξα τῇ ἁγίᾳ Ἀναστάσει σου Κύριε*), ἀλλὰ μόνον εἰς τὰ δύο τελευταῖα τὸ *Δόξα, Καὶ νῦν*· οὐδὲν ἧττον ὅμως καθ᾽ ἁπάσας τὰς μεθεόρτους ἡμέρας ψαλλομένου τοῦ Κανόνος τῆς προηγηθείσης Δεσποτικῆς ἑορτῆς, προτάττεται τὸ *Δόξα σοι ὁ Θεὸς ἡμῶν δόξα σοι*.

---

## ΠΕΡΙ ΤΟΥ ΕΝ ΤΗ Γ΄ ΩΔΗ ΚΑΘΙΣΜΑΤΟΣ

**25.** Ἐν ταῖς Κυριακαῖς, μετὰ τὴν τῆς γ΄ Ὠδῆς Αἴτησιν, ψάλλεται τὸ Κάθισμα καὶ τὸ Θεοτοκίον τοῦ ἐπιλαχόντος Ἁγίου· Θεομητορικῆς δὲ ἑορτῆς τυχούσης, προαναγινώσκεται τὸ Κοντάκιον καὶ ὁ Οἶκος τῆς Ὀκτωήχου, καὶ εἶτα ψάλλεται τὸ Κάθισμα τῆς ἑορτῆς ἅπαξ· τὸ αὐτὸ γίνεται καὶ κατὰ πάσας τὰς Κυριακὰς τοῦ Τριῳδίου καὶ ἐν ταῖς μνήμαις τῶν ἐπισημότερον ἑορταζομένων Ἁγίων.
  Ἐν δὲ ταῖς προεορτίοις καὶ μεθεόρτοις Κυριακαῖς πρῶτον ψάλλεται τὸ τοῦ τυχόντος Ἁγίου Κάθισμα, εἶτα τὸ προεόρτιον ἢ μεθέορτον.

---

## ΠΕΡΙ ΚΟΝΤΑΚΙΟΥ ΚΑΙ ΟΙΚΟΥ

**26.** Ἐν ταῖς Κυριακαῖς μετὰ τὴν τῆς ς΄ Ὠδῆς τοῦ Κανόνος Αἴτησιν (ἥτις γίνεται ὑπὸ τοῦ Ἱερέως μὲ τὴν ἐκφώνησιν *Ὅτι Σὺ εἶ ὁ Βασιλεὺς τῆς εἰρήνης*), ἀναγινώσκονται τὸ Κοντάκιον καὶ ὁ Οἶκος τῆς Ὀκτωήχου· ἐξαιροῦνται αἱ Κυριακαί, ἐν αἷς συμπίπτουσι Θεομητορικαὶ ἑορταὶ καὶ πᾶσαι αἱ τοῦ Τριῳδίου (πλὴν τῆς Β΄, Δ΄ καὶ Ε΄ Κυριακῆς τῶν Νηστειῶν, καθ᾽ ἃς ἀναγινώσκονται τὰ τῆς Ὀκτωήχου)·

*(Printed Page 72)*

=== LEAF p88 ===

...of Thy servants"** (*Πρεσβείαις τῶν Μαρτύρων σου, Χριστὲ ὁ Θεός, ἀνάπαυσον τὰς ψυχὰς τῶν δούλων σου*); for the Funeral Troparia: **"Blessed are they whom Thou hast chosen and taken to Thyself, O Lord"** (*Μακάριοι οὓς ἐξελέξω καὶ προσελάβου, Κύριε*, Psalm 64:5 LXX), or **"Their souls shall dwell among good things"** (*Αἱ ψυχαὶ αὐτῶν ἐν ἀγαθοῖς αὐλισθήσονται*, Psalm 24:13 LXX), or **"And their memorial is from generation to generation"** (*Καὶ τὸ μνημόσυνον αὐτῶν εἰς γενεὰν καὶ γενεάν*, Psalm 101:13 LXX). For the Penitential Troparia (*Katanyktika*) of the Parakletike, of the Triodion, as well as of Forefeasts and Afterfeasts of Feasts of the Lord: **"Glory to Thee, our God, glory to Thee"** (*Δόξα σοι ὁ Θεὸς ἡμῶν, δόξα σοι*); and for the Martyrika of the Parakletike: **"Saints of God, intercede for us"** (*Ἅγιοι τοῦ Θεοῦ, πρεσβεύσατε ὑπὲρ ἡμῶν*). Note that since, on the great Feasts of the Lord, the Nine Biblical Canticles are not chanted in verses according to the ancient typikon order, for this reason no verse is chanted before the troparia of all Feasts of the Lord (except Easter [Pascha], when we chant **"Glory to Thy holy Resurrection, O Lord"**); but only for the last two troparia do we chant **"Glory to the Father, and to the Son, and to the Holy Spirit"**, **"Now and forever, and unto the ages of ages. Amen."** Nevertheless, on all Afterfeast days, when chanting the Canon of the preceding Feast of the Lord, the verse **"Glory to Thee, our God, glory to Thee"** is prefixed before its troparia.[^86]

---

### Concerning the Kathisma [Sessional Hymn] of the Third Ode

**25.** On Sundays, after the Little Litany following the Third Ode of the Canon, the Kathisma and Theotokion of the occurring Saint are chanted. If a Feast of the Mother of God occurs, the Kontakion and Ikos of the Octoechos are read beforehand, and then the Kathisma of the feast is chanted once; the same order is observed on all Sundays of the Triodion and on the commemorations of more solemnly celebrated Saints.
  On Forefeast and Afterfeast Sundays, the Kathisma of the occurring Saint is chanted first, and then that of the Forefeast or Afterfeast.[^87]

---

### Concerning the Kontakion and Ikos

**26.** On Sundays at Matins, after the Little Litany following the Sixth Ode of the Canon (which is intoned by the Priest with the exclamation **"For Thou art the King of peace, and the Savior of our souls, and unto Thee do we send up glory..."** [*Ὅτι Σὺ εἶ ὁ Βασιλεὺς τῆς εἰρήνης...*]), the Kontakion and Ikos of the Octoechos are read. Excepted are Sundays on which Feasts of the Mother of God occur, as well as all Sundays of the Triodion (except the Second, Fourth, and Fifth Sundays of Lent, on which the Kontakion and Ikos of the Octoechos are read), the Sundays of the Pentecostarion, the Sunday of the Holy Forefathers, the Sunday before the Nativity of Christ, the Sunday after the Nativity of Christ, the three Sundays...

*(Printed Page 73)*

=== LEAF p89 ===

...καὶ αἱ τοῦ Πεντηκοσταρίου, ἡ Κυριακὴ τῶν ἁγίων Προπατόρων, ἡ πρὸ τῆς Χριστοῦ Γεννήσεως, ἡ μετὰ τὴν Χριστοῦ Γέννησιν, αἱ τρεῖς Κυριακαὶ τῶν ἁγίων Πατέρων τῆς Α΄, Δ΄ καὶ Ζ΄ Συνόδου καὶ ἡ τῶν ἁγίων Πάντων, καθ᾽ ἃς ἀναγινώσκονται τὰ τῶν ἑορτῶν Κοντάκια καὶ οἱ Οἶκοι· ἐπίσης ἐξαιροῦνται καὶ αἱ ἐφεξῆς ἑορταί, τοῦ Γενεσίου τοῦ Προδρόμου, τῶν ἀποστόλων Πέτρου καὶ Παύλου, τοῦ Εὐαγγελιστοῦ Ἰωάννου (Σεπτεμ. κς΄), τοῦ ἀποστόλου Ἀνδρέου, τῶν τριῶν Ἱεραρχῶν (ὅταν συμπίπτῃ ἡ μνήμη αὐτῶν ἐν Κυριακῇ ἐκτὸς τοῦ Τριῳδίου), τῶν Ταξιαρχῶν καὶ τῶν ἁγίων Δημητρίου καὶ Νικολάου, καθ᾽ ἃς προαναγινώσκεται (ὡς εἴρηται ἐν §25) τὸ Ἀναστάσιμον Κοντάκιον μετὰ τοῦ Οἴκου ἐν τῇ γ΄ Ὠδῇ πρὸ τοῦ Καθίσματος. Ἀναγινώσκονται ὡσαύτως μετὰ τὴν ς΄ Ὠδὴν ἀντὶ τῶν Ἀναστασίμων τὰ Κοντάκια καὶ οἱ Οἶκοι τῶν ἁγίων Γεωργίου, Ἰωάννου τοῦ Θεολόγου (Μαΐου η΄) καὶ Κωνσταντίνου καὶ Ἑλένης, ὅταν συμπίπτωσιν ἔν τινι Κυριακῇ τοῦ Πεντηκοσταρίου, πλὴν τῆς Κυριακῆς τοῦ Θωμᾶ, τῆς Πεντηκοστῆς καὶ τῶν ἁγίων Πάντων.

## ΠΕΡΙ ΤΩΝ ΚΑΤΑΒΑΣΙΩΝ

**27.** Εἰς πάσας τὰς Κυριακὰς τὰς μεταξὺ τῶν ἐφεξῆς περιόδων περιλαμβανομένας, ἤτοι ἀπὸ τῆς Ἀποδόσεως τῆς ἑορτῆς τοῦ Τιμίου Σταυροῦ μέχρι τῆς κα΄ Νοεμβρίου,—ἀπὸ τῆς Ἀποδόσεως τῆς ἑορτῆς τῆς Ὑπαπαντῆς μέχρι τῆς ἐνάρξεως τοῦ Τριῳδίου, ὡς καὶ τὴν Β΄, Δ΄ καὶ Ε΄ Κυριακὴν τῶν Νηστειῶν,—ἀπὸ τῆς Κυριακῆς τῶν ἁγίων Πάντων μέχρι τῆς κζ΄ Ἰουλίου καὶ εἰς πάσας τὰς ἐντὸς τῆς ἑβδομάδος τῶν περιόδων τούτων συμπιπτούσας Ἁγίων ἑορταζομένων μνήμας, ψάλλονται Καταβασίαι *Ἀνοίξω τὸ στόμα μου*. Εἰς τὰς μεταξὺ τῶν προεορτίων καὶ μεθεόρτων τῶν Δεσποτικῶν καὶ Θεομητορικῶν ἑορτῶν συμπιπτούσας Κυριακάς, καὶ τὰς ἐντὸς τῆς ἑβδομάδος τυχούσας Ἁγίων ἑορταζομένων μνήμας, ψάλλονται αἱ Καταβασίαι τῆς τυχούσης Δεσποτικῆς ἢ Θεομητορικῆς ἑορτῆς. Εἰς τὰς πρώτας τέσσαρας Κυριακὰς τοῦ Τριῳδίου (ἐὰν μὴ ἀπεδόθη ἡ ἑορτὴ τῆς Ὑπαπαντῆς) ψάλλονται αἱ τοῦ Τριῳδίου Καταβασίαι, ὡς καὶ τὴν Α΄ Κυριακὴν τῶν Νηστειῶν. Ἐν οἵᾳ δήποτε δὲ ἡμέρᾳ ἄλλῃ πλὴν τῆς Κυριακῆς τῶν ἑβδομάδων τοῦ Τριῳδίου, συμπιπτούσης ἑορτῆς Ἁγίου τινός, ψάλλονται Καταβασίαι *Ἀνοίξω τὸ στόμα μου*, καὶ Κοντάκιον ἐν τῇ Λειτουργίᾳ *Προστασία τῶν Χριστιανῶν*.

*(Printed Page 74)*

=== LEAF p90 ===

...of the Holy Fathers of the First, Fourth, and Seventh Ecumenical Councils, and the Sunday of All Saints, on which the Kontakion and Ikos of the Feast are read. Also excepted are the following feasts: the Nativity of the Forerunner, the Apostles Peter and Paul, John the Evangelist (September 26), the Apostle Andrew, the Three Hierarchs (when their commemoration falls on a Sunday outside the Triodion period), the Archangels [Archistrategoi Michael and Gabriel], and Saints Demetrius and Nicholas, on which feasts the Resurrection Kontakion and Ikos are read beforehand at the Third Ode before the Kathisma (as stated above in §25). Likewise, after the Sixth Ode, instead of the Resurrectional hymns, the Kontakia and Ikoi of Saints George, John the Theologian (May 8), and Constantine and Helen are read whenever these coincide on any Sunday of the Pentecostarion, except Thomas Sunday, Pentecost, and All Saints.[^88]

## Concerning the Katavasiae

**27.** On all Sundays included in the intervals between the following periods—namely, from the Apodosis of the Feast of the Precious Cross [September 21] until November 21; from the Apodosis of the Feast of the Meeting of the Lord [February 9] until the beginning of the Triodion, as well as on the Second, Fourth, and Fifth Sundays of Great Lent; from the Sunday of All Saints until July 27; and on all commemorations of celebrated Saints coinciding during the weekdays of these periods—the Katavasiae **"I shall open my mouth"** (*Ἀνοίξω τὸ στόμα μου*) are chanted. On Sundays falling between the Forefeasts and Afterfeasts of Feasts of the Lord and Feasts of the Mother of God, and on commemorations of celebrated Saints occurring during the week, the Katavasiae of the occurring Feast of the Lord or Mother of God are chanted. On the first four Sundays of the Triodion (if the Feast of the Meeting of the Lord has not undergone its Apodosis), the Katavasiae of the Triodion are chanted, as also on the First Sunday of Lent [Triumph of Orthodoxy]. On whatever day of the week other than Sunday during the weeks of the Triodion the commemoration of a Saint occurs, the Katavasiae **"I shall open my mouth"** are chanted, and at the Divine Liturgy the Kontakion **"O Protection of Christians unashamed"** (*Προστασία τῶν Χριστιανῶν ἀκαταίσχυντε*).[^89]

*(Printed Page 75)*

## ΠΕΡΙ ΤΟΥ ΕΝ ΤΩι ΟΡΘΡΩι ΤΗΣ ΚΥΡΙΑΚΗΣ
### ΕΩΘΙΝΟΥ ΕΥΑΓΓΕΛΙΟΥ

**28.** Ἐν ταῖς Κυριακαῖς, μετὰ τὴν Καταβασίαν τῆς η΄ Ὠδῆς, ὁ Διάκονος (ἢ ὁ Ἱερεὺς ἐν ἐλλείψει Διακόνου) ἐκφωνεῖ, *Τοῦ Κυρίου δεηθῶμεν*· ὁ Χορὸς *Κύριε ἐλέησον* τρίς· ὁ Ἱερεὺς *Ὅτι Ἅγιος εἶ ὁ Θεὸς ἡμῶν καὶ Ἁγίοις ἐπαναπαύῃ...* οἱ Χοροὶ τὸ *Πᾶσα πνοή*, καὶ ὁ Ἱερεὺς λέγει τὸ ἐνδιάτακτον Ἑωθινὸν Εὐαγγέλιον, μεθ’ ὃ ἕπεται τὸ *Ἀνάστασιν Χριστοῦ θεασάμενοι* καὶ ψάλλεται ὁ Ν΄ ψαλμὸς *Ἐλέησόν με ὁ Θεός*, *Δόξα* *Ταῖς τῶν Ἀποστόλων*, *Καὶ νῦν* *Ταῖς τῆς Θεοτόκου*, ὁ Στίχος *Ἐλέησόν με ὁ Θεὸς* καὶ τὸ *Ἀναστὰς ὁ Ἰησοῦς*. Εἶτα ὁ Ἱερεὺς10 *Σῶσον ὁ Θεὸς τὸν λαόν σου... Ἐλέει καὶ οἰκτιρμοῖς...* Ἐν δὲ ταῖς τοῦ Τριῳδίου Κυριακαῖς μετὰ τὸν Ν΄ ἀντὶ τοῦ *Δόξα* *Ταῖς τῶν Ἀποστόλων*, ψάλλονται τὰ Ἰδιόμελα τοῦ Τριῳδίου, *Δόξα* *Τῆς μετανοίας ἄνοιξόν μοι*, *Καὶ νῦν* *Τῆς σωτηρίας εὔθυνόν μοι*, ὁ στίχος *Ἐλέησόν με ὁ Θεὸς* καὶ τὸ Ἰδιόμελον *Τὰ πλήθη τῶν πεπραγμένων μοι δεινῶν*, τὸ *Σῶσον ὁ Θεὸς τὸν λαόν σου*, καὶ ἀκολούθως στιχολογεῖται τὸ *Τὴν Τιμιωτέραν*.

---

## ΠΕΡΙ ΤΗΣ ΕΝ ΤΗι Θ΄ ΩΔΗι ΣΤΙΧΟΛΟΓΙΑΣ
### ΤΟΥ ΜΕΓΑΛΥΝΕΙ Η ΨΥΧΗ ΜΟΥ

**29.** Καθ’ ἑκάστην ἡμέραν καὶ Κυριακήν, καὶ κατὰ πάσας τῶν ἑορταζομένων Ἁγίων μνήμας, κατὰ τὰς μεθεόρτους τῶν Δεσποτικῶν καὶ Θεομητορικῶν ἑορτῶν Κυριακάς, καὶ τὰς μετὰ τὴν Κυριακὴν τοῦ Θωμᾶ μέχρι τῆς Πεντηκοστῆς ἐντὸς τῶν λοιπῶν, πλὴν τῆς Κυριακῆς, ἡμερῶν τυχούσας Ἁγίων ἑορταζομένων μνήμας, στιχολογοῦμεν *Τὴν Τιμιωτέραν* εἰς τὸν ἦχον τῶν ἐπιλαχουσῶν Καταβασιῶν. Ἐν ταῖς μεγάλαις Δεσποτικαῖς καὶ Θεομητορικαῖς ἑορταῖς, ἐν οἱᾳδήποτε...

10. Τὸ *Σῶσον ὁ Θεὸς* ἀνήκει τῷ Ἱερεῖ κατὰ τὴν ἀρχαίαν τυπικὴν διάταξιν· ἀλλ᾽ ἐπειδὴ συμβαίνει νὰ τελῇ τότε τὴν Προσκομιδήν, ἵνα μὴ διακόψῃ αὐτήν, ἐπετράπη φαίνεται καὶ τῷ Διακόνῳ λέγειν ταῦτα· ἡ τάξις ὅμως τηρητέα, καὶ ὁ Ἱερεὺς ὀφείλει λαμβάνων ὑπ’ ὄψει ταύτην, νὰ σταματήσῃ εἴς τι σημεῖον τὴν Προσκομιδὴν διὰ νὰ εἰπῇ τὴν εὐχὴν τοῦ *Σῶσον ὁ Θεός*.
